Third, we develop a mask-guided inference strategy that ensures precise style alignment between generated and reference text. To facilitate systematic evaluation, we construct StyleText-CE, a bilingual scene text style benchmark covering both monolingual and cross-lingual settings. Extensive experiments demonstrate that StyleTextGen significantly outperforms existing methods in style consistency and cross-lingual generalization, establishing new state-of-the-art performance in multilingual style-conditioned text generation.

中文摘要翻译: 风格条件场景文本生成在从复杂背景中提取精确文本风格以及保持字符间细粒度风格一致性方面面临独特挑战，尤其是在多语言脚本场景下。本文提出StyleTextGen，一种新颖的框架，能够学习感知和复制不同语言和书写系统中的视觉文本风格。我们的方法包含三项关键贡献：首先，引入一个专用于风格建模的双分支风格编码器，在复杂真实场景中生成鲁棒的多语言文本风格表示。其次，设计了一个文本风格一致性损失，增强风格连贯性并改善整体视觉质量。第三，开发了一种掩码引导推理策略，确保生成文本与参考文本之间的精确风格对齐。为促进系统评估，我们构建了StyleText-CE，一个涵盖单语和跨语言设置的双语场景文本风格基准。大量实验表明，StyleTextGen在风格一致性和跨语言泛化方面显著优于现有方法，在多语言风格条件文本生成中树立了新的最先进性能。

</details>


### [61] [Breaking Dual Bottlenecks: Evolving Unified Multimodal Models into Self-Adaptive Interleaved Visual Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14709)
*Qingyang Liu,Bingjie Gao,Canmiao Fu,Zhipeng Huang,Chen Li,Feng Wang,Shuochen Chang,Shaobo Wang,Yali Wang,Keming Ye,Jiangtong Li,Li Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应框架，让统一模型根据指令复杂度在直接生成、自我反思和多步规划三种策略间自主切换，以弥合多模态理解与生成之间的鸿沟，在X2I任务上超越现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有统一模型在理解与生成之间存在“理解-生成鸿沟”：模型能理解用户意图，但难以将语义知识转化为精确的像素级操作。具体表现为两个瓶颈：注意力纠缠瓶颈（盲目规划难以处理复杂指令）和视觉细化瓶颈（无结构反馈无法高效修正缺陷）。

Method: 构建分层数据流水线，支持三种自适应模式：简单指令直接生成、质量细化自我反思、复杂场景多步规划。基于此创建5万+样本的高质量数据集，设计两阶段训练策略（SFT+RL），其中包含步骤级推理奖励（保证逻辑一致性）和组内复杂度惩罚（防止冗余计算开销）。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在任意图到图像(X2I)任务上优于现有基线，在简单到复杂指令下均实现了更优的生成保真度。

Conclusion: 通过自适应策略切换机制和分层数据流水线，有效弥合了统一模型中的理解-生成鸿沟，为提升多模态模型的像素级操控能力提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Recent unified models integrate multimodal understanding and generation within a single framework. However, an "understanding-generation gap" persists, where models can capture user intent but often fail to translate this semantic knowledge into precise pixel-level manipulation. This gap results in two bottlenecks in anything-to-image task (X2I): the attention entanglement bottleneck, where blind planning struggles with complex prompts, and the visual refinement bottleneck, where unstructured feedback fails to correct imperfections efficiently. In this paper, we propose a novel framework that empowers unified models to autonomously switch between generation strategies based on instruction complexity and model capability. To achieve this, we construct a hierarchical data pipeline that constructs execution paths across three adaptive modes: direct generation for simple cases, self-reflection for quality refinement, and multi-step planning for decomposing complex scenarios. Building on this pipeline, we contribute a high-quality dataset with over 50,000 samples and implement a two-stage training strategy comprising SFT and RL. Specifically, we design step-wise reasoning rewards to ensure logical consistency and intra-group complexity penalty to prevent redundant computational overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing baselines on X2I, achieving superior generation fidelity among simple-to-complex instructions. The code is released at https://github.com/WeChatCV/Interleaved_Visual_Reasoner.

中文摘要翻译: 近期统一模型将多模态理解与生成整合在单一框架中。然而，“理解-生成鸿沟”仍然存在：模型能够捕捉用户意图，但往往难以将这种语义知识转化为精确的像素级操作。这一鸿沟在任意图到图像任务（X2I）中导致了两个瓶颈：注意力纠缠瓶颈（盲目规划难以处理复杂提示）和视觉细化瓶颈（无结构反馈无法高效修正缺陷）。在本文中，我们提出一种新颖框架，使统一模型能够根据指令复杂度和模型能力自主切换生成策略。为实现这一目标，我们构建了一个分层数据流水线，支持三种自适应模式的执行路径：简单场景的直接生成、质量细化的自我反思，以及复杂场景分解的多步规划。在此流水线基础上，我们贡献了一个包含5万余样本的高质量数据集，并实施了包含监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）的两阶段训练策略。具体而言，我们设计了步骤级推理奖励以保证逻辑一致性，以及组内复杂度惩罚以防止冗余计算开销。大量实验表明，我们的方法在X2I任务上优于现有基线，在从简单到复杂的指令下均实现了更优的生成保真度。代码已开源至 https://github.com/WeChatCV/Interleaved_Visual_Reasoner。

</details>


### [62] [Vision-Core Guided Contrastive Learning for Balanced Multi-modal Prognosis Prediction of Stroke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14710)
*Liren Chen,Lidong Sun,Mingyan Huang,Junzhe Tang,Yinghui Zhu,Guanjie Wang,Yiqing Xia,Ting Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向缺血性卒中预后预测的新型三模态融合模型，利用大语言模型从脑部MRI自动生成半结构化诊断文本以增强数据表示，并设计视觉条件双对齐融合模块实现图像、结构化临床数据与文本的深度双向交互，在真实临床数据集上取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学多模态融合方法在缺血性卒中预后方面存在两大局限：一是仅局限于双模态融合，缺乏整合医学影像、结构化临床数据与非结构化文本三模态的统一框架；二是未能建立模态间的深层双向交互机制。

Method: 首先利用大语言模型从脑部MRI自动生成半结构化诊断文本，解决专家标注稀缺问题并提升多模态融合鲁棒性；其次设计视觉条件双对齐融合模块，以视觉特征作为条件先验引导与生成文本的细粒度交互，通过双重语义对齐损失实现动态深度融合。

Result: 在真实世界临床数据集上的大量实验表明，该模型达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的三模态融合框架有效弥补了现有方法在模态覆盖和交互深度上的不足，为缺血性卒中预后预测提供了一种更准确、鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Deep learning and multi-modal fusion have demonstrated transformative potential in medical diagnosis by integrating diverse data sources. However, accurate prognosis for ischemic stroke remains challenging due to limitations in existing multi-modal approaches. First, current methods are predominantly confined to dual-modal fusion, lacking a framework that effectively integrates the trifecta of medical images, structured clinical data, and unstructured text. Second, they often fail to establish deep bidirectional interactions between modalities; To address these critical gaps, this paper proposes a novel tri-modal fusion model for ischemic stroke prognosis. Our approach first enriches the data representation by employing a Large Language Model (LLM) to automatically generate semi-structured diagnostic text from brain MRIs. This process not only addresses the scarcity of expert annotations but also serves as a regularized semantic enhancement, improving multimodal fusion robustness. Furthermore, we design a core component termed the Vision-Conditioned Dual Alignment Fusion Module (VDAFM), which strategically uses visual features as a conditional prior to guide fine-grained interaction with the generated text. This module achieves a dynamic and profound fusion through a dual semantic alignment loss, effectively mitigating modal heterogeneity. Extensive experiments on a real-world clinical dataset demonstrate that our model achieves state-of-the-art performance.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习和多模态融合通过整合多样化的数据源，在医学诊断中展现出了变革性的潜力。然而，由于现有多模态方法的局限性，缺血性卒中的准确预后仍然面临挑战。首先，当前方法主要局限于双模态融合，缺乏能够有效整合医学影像、结构化临床数据和非结构化文本这三类数据源的有效框架。其次，现有方法往往无法建立模态间的深层双向交互。为填补这些关键空白，本文提出了一种面向缺血性卒中预后预测的新型三模态融合模型。我们的方法首先利用大语言模型从脑部MRI自动生成半结构化诊断文本，以丰富数据表示。这一过程不仅解决了专家标注稀缺的问题，还作为一种正则化的语义增强手段，提升了多模态融合的鲁棒性。此外，我们设计了名为视觉条件双对齐融合模块的核心组件，该模块策略性地将视觉特征作为条件先验，引导与生成文本的细粒度交互。该模块通过双重语义对齐损失实现动态且深度的融合，有效缓解了模态异质性。在真实世界临床数据集上进行的大量实验表明，我们的模型取得了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [63] [Towards Label-Free Single-Cell Phenotyping Using Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14717)
*Saqib Nazir,Ardhendu Behera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的深度学习框架，从无标记差分相衬图像中同时进行白细胞分类和连续蛋白表达回归，并利用大语言模型生成生物学解释。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无标记单细胞成像虽具可扩展性和非侵入性优势，但直接从明场形态推断分子表型仍然困难，亟需一种无需荧光标记即可同时识别细胞表型和定量生物标志物的方法。

Method: 采用混合架构，通过可学习的跨分支门控模块融合卷积细粒度纹理特征与Transformer全局表示，实现从DPC图像的形态-分子联合推断；并引入大语言模型生成细胞状态的生物学解释。

Result: 在BSCCM数据集上，白细胞分类准确率达91.3%，CD16表达回归的Pearson相关系数为0.72，在多个基准上表现优越。

Conclusion: 无标记单细胞成像结合深度学习可有效实现低成本血液学分析，同时完成表型识别和定量生物标志物估计，无需荧光染色。

Original Abstract: Label-free single-cell imaging offers a scalable, non-invasive alternative to fluorescence-based cytometry, yet inferring molecular phenotypes directly from bright-field morphology remains challenging. We present a unified Deep Learning (DL) framework that jointly performs White Blood Cell (WBC) classification and continuous protein-expression regression from label-free Differential Phase Contrast (DPC) images. Our model employs a Hybrid architecture that fuses convolutional fine-grained texture features with transformer-based global representations through a learnable cross-branch gating module, enabling robust morpho-molecular inference from DPC images. To support downstream interpretability, we further incorporate a Large Language Model (LLM) that generates concise, biologically grounded summaries of the predicted cell states. Experiments on the Berkeley Single Cell Computational Microscopy (BSCCM) and Blood Cells Image benchmarks demonstrate strong performance, achieving a 91.3% WBC classification accuracy and a 0.72 Pearson correlation for CD16 expression regression on BSCCM. These results underscore the promise of label-free single-cell imaging for cost-effective hematological profiling, enabling simultaneous phenotype identification and quantitative biomarker estimation without fluorescent staining. The source code is available at https://github.com/saqibnaziir/Single-Cell-Phenotyping.

中文摘要翻译: 无标记单细胞成像为基于荧光的细胞计数提供了一种可扩展、非侵入性的替代方案，然而直接从明场形态推断分子表型仍具有挑战性。我们提出了一个统一的深度学习框架，能够从无标记差分相衬图像中同时进行白细胞分类和连续蛋白表达回归。我们的模型采用混合架构，通过可学习的跨分支门控模块融合卷积细粒度纹理特征与基于Transformer的全局表示，从而实现对DPC图像的稳健形态-分子推断。为支持下游可解释性，我们进一步引入大语言模型，生成简洁且具有生物学依据的预测细胞状态总结。在Berkeley单细胞计算显微镜和血细胞图像基准上的实验表明，本方法性能优异，在BSCCM上实现了91.3%的白细胞分类准确率和0.72的CD16表达回归皮尔逊相关系数。这些结果凸显了无标记单细胞成像在低成本血液学分析中的潜力，能够在无需荧光染色的条件下同时实现表型识别和定量生物标志物估计。源代码可在https://github.com/saqibnaziir/Single-Cell-Phenotyping获取。

</details>


### [64] [Video-Zero: Self-Evolution Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14733)
*Ruixu Zhang,Deyi Ji,Lanyun Zhu,Xuanyi Liu,Yuxin Meng,Ruihang Chu,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Video-Zero，一个无需标注的问答者-求解者协同进化框架，通过将视频自进化的核心从“难度”转向“证据基础”，在时间定位证据上生成有依据的问题并训练模型，在13个视频基准上持续提升多种视频VLM骨干模型的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频自进化方法简单从全视频生成困难问答对，但生成的监督信号虽难却弱接地，依赖静态线索或语言先验而非时序证据。作者认为视频自进化的关键瓶颈不是难度本身，而是证据基础（grounding）。

Method: 提出Video-Zero，一个无需人工标注的Questioner-Solver协同进化框架。Questioner负责发现信息性证据片段并生成基于证据的问题，Solver学习回答问题并将其预测与支持性证据对齐，形成证据发现、有依据监督、证据对齐学习的迭代闭环。

Result: 在涵盖时间定位、长视频理解和视频推理的13个基准上，Video-Zero持续改进了多种视频VLM骨干模型，证明了以证据为中心的自进化的有效性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: 视频自进化的关键瓶颈在于证据基础而非难度，以证据为中心的自进化框架能有效提升视频理解模型的推理能力。

Original Abstract: Self-evolution offers a promising path for improving reasoning models without relying on intensive human annotation. However, extending this paradigm to video understanding remains underexplored and challenging: videos are long, dynamic, and redundant, while the evidence needed for reasoning is often sparse and temporally localized. Naively generating difficult question-answer pairs from full videos can therefore produce supervision that appears challenging but is weakly grounded, relying on static cues or language priors rather than temporal evidence. In this work, we argue that the key bottleneck of video self-evolution is not difficulty alone, but grounding. We propose Video-Zero, an annotation-free Questioner--Solver co-evolution framework that centers self-evolution on temporally localized evidence. The Questioner discovers informative evidence segments and generates evidence-grounded questions, while the Solver learns to answer and align its predictions with the supporting evidence. This closes an iterative loop of evidence discovery, grounded supervision, and evidence-aligned learning. Across 13 benchmarks spanning temporal grounding, long-video understanding, and video reasoning, Video-Zero consistently improves multiple video VLM backbones, demonstrating the effectiveness and transferability of evidence-centered self-evolution.

中文摘要翻译: 自进化为改进推理模型提供了一条有前景的路径，无需依赖大量人工标注。然而，将该范式扩展到视频理解领域仍未被充分探索且充满挑战：视频长、动态且冗余，而推理所需的证据往往是稀疏且时间上局部化的。从整个视频中天真地生成困难问答对，所产生的监督信号虽然看似具有挑战性，但却是弱接地的——依赖于静态线索或语言先验而非时序证据。在本文中，我们认为视频自进化的关键瓶颈不在于难度本身，而在于证据基础（grounding）。为此，我们提出Video-Zero，一种无需标注的问答者-求解者协同进化框架，将自进化的核心聚焦于时间定位的证据。问答者发现信息性证据片段并生成基于证据的问题，求解者学习回答问题并将其预测与支持性证据对齐。这形成了一个包含证据发现、有依据监督和证据对齐学习的迭代闭环。在涵盖时间定位、长视频理解和视频推理的13个基准上，Video-Zero持续改进了多种视频VLM骨干模型，证明了以证据为中心的自进化的有效性和可迁移性。

</details>


### [65] [EARL: Towards a Unified Analysis-Guided Reinforcement Learning Framework for Egocentric Interaction Reasoning and Pixel Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14742)
*Yuejiao Su,Xinshen Zhang,Zhen Ye,Lei Yao,Lap-Pui Chau,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EARL 是一种面向第一人称视觉交互的强化学习框架，通过两阶段解析（粗粒度场景理解+细粒度查询响应）和全局交互描述子，将粗粒度的交互语义显式迁移到查询导向的问答与像素级分割中，在Ego-IRGBench上达到65.48% cIoU，显著优于此前基于RL的方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在处理第一人称（自我中心）视觉中的交互推理和细粒度像素级定位时仍存在不足，亟需一种能显式利用交互语义来提升查询导向响应与定位精度的方法。

Method: EARL采用两阶段解析框架：第一阶段对自我中心交互进行整体理解，生成结构化的文本描述；第二阶段输出文本答案和像素级掩码。两阶段之间通过提取全局交互描述子作为语义先验，并利用新提出的分析引导特征合成器（AFS）进行融合。为优化文本答案、边界框和分割掩码等异构输出，设计了多层面奖励函数，结合GRPO算法训练响应阶段。

Result: 在Ego-IRGBench上，EARL的像素级分割cIoU达到65.48%，比此前基于RL的方法高出8.37%；在EgoHOS上的OOD（分布外）定位结果表明其具有较强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: EARL通过显式迁移交互语义到查询导向任务中，有效提升了第一人称视觉的交互推理与像素级定位性能，并在未见过的自我中心场景中具有良好的迁移性。

Original Abstract: Understanding human--environment interactions from egocentric vision is essential for assistive robotics and embodied intelligent agents, yet existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs) still struggle with accurate interaction reasoning and fine-grained pixel grounding. To this end, this paper introduces EARL, an Egocentric Analysis-guided Reinforcement Learning framework that explicitly transfers coarse interaction semantics to query-oriented answering and grounding. Specifically, EARL adopts a two-stage parsing framework including coarse-grained interpretation and fine-grained response. The first stage holistically interprets egocentric interactions and generates a structured textual description. The second stage produces the textual answer and pixel-level mask in response to the user query. To bridge the two stages, we extract a global interaction descriptor as a semantic prior, which is integrated via a novel Analysis-guided Feature Synthesizer (AFS) for query-oriented reasoning. To optimize heterogeneous outputs, including textual answers, bounding boxes, and grounding masks, we design a multi-faceted reward function and train the response stage with GRPO. Experiments on Ego-IRGBench show that EARL achieves 65.48% cIoU for pixel grounding, outperforming previous RL-based methods by 8.37%, while OOD grounding results on EgoHOS indicate strong transferability to unseen egocentric grounding scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 理解第一人称视角下的人-环境交互对于辅助机器人和具身智能体至关重要，然而现有的大规模多模态语言模型在准确的交互推理和细粒度像素级定位方面仍存在困难。为此，本文提出了EARL，一种基于自我中心分析引导的强化学习框架，能够将粗粒度的交互语义显式迁移到查询导向的回答与定位任务中。具体来说，EARL采用两阶段解析框架，包括粗粒度解释和细粒度响应。第一阶段整体解读自我中心交互并生成结构化的文本描述；第二阶段根据用户查询生成文本答案和像素级掩码。为桥接这两个阶段，我们提取全局交互描述子作为语义先验，并通过一种新提出的分析引导特征合成器（AFS）将其融入查询导向的推理过程。为优化包含文本答案、边界框和分割掩码在内的异构输出，我们设计了多层面奖励函数，并采用GRPO训练响应阶段。在Ego-IRGBench上的实验表明，EARL在像素级定位上达到65.48%的cIoU，比此前基于RL的方法高出8.37%，同时在EgoHOS上的分布外定位结果展示了其在未见过的自我中心定位场景中强大的可迁移性。

</details>


### [66] [BioHuman: Learning Biomechanical Human Representations from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14772)
*Yujun Huo,He Zhang,Chentao Song,Honglin Song,Zongyu Zuo,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于仿真框架的大规模生物力学数据集BioHuman10M，以及端到端模型BioHuman，能够从单目视频直接预测人体运动与肌肉激活状态，实现从视觉观察到内部生物力学状态的推断。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法无法直接从视觉观测中推断人体内部生物力学状态（如肌肉激活），且缺乏带有生物力学标注的大规模数据集，限制了人体运动分析、康复和损伤风险评估等领域的发展。

Method: 1) 构建仿真框架，从现有动作捕捉数据集中估计肌肉激活，生成包含同步视频、运动与肌肉激活的大规模数据集BioHuman10M；2) 提出端到端模型BioHuman，以单目视频为输入，联合预测人体运动与肌肉激活。

Result: 实验表明BioHuman能够准确重建运动学运动和肌肉活动，并在不同被试和动作类型上具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法为基于视频的生物力学理解建立了新基准，为物理上可信的人体建模开辟了新可能。

Original Abstract: Understanding human motion beyond surface kinematics is crucial for motion analysis, rehabilitation, and injury risk assessment. However, progress in this domain is limited by the lack of large-scale datasets with biomechanical annotations, and by existing approaches that cannot directly infer internal biomechanical states from visual observations. In this paper, we introduce a simulation-based framework for estimating muscle activations from existing motion capture datasets, resulting in BioHuman10M, a large-scale dataset with synchronized video, motion, and activations. Building on BioHuman10M, we propose BioHuman, an end-to-end model that takes monocular video as input and jointly predicts human motion and muscle activations, effectively bridging visual observations and internal biomechanical states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BioHuman enables accurate reconstruction of both kinematic motion and muscle activity, and generalizes across diverse subjects and motions. We believe our approach establishes a new benchmark for video-based biomechanical understanding and opens up new possibilities for physically grounded human modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 理解超越表面运动学的人体运动对于运动分析、康复和损伤风险评估至关重要。然而，该领域的进展受到缺乏带有生物力学标注的大规模数据集以及现有方法无法直接从视觉观测中推断内部生物力学状态的限制。本文提出了一种基于仿真框架的方法，从现有动作捕捉数据集中估计肌肉激活，从而构建了BioHuman10M——一个包含同步视频、运动与肌肉激活的大规模数据集。基于BioHuman10M，我们提出了BioHuman，一个端到端模型，以单目视频为输入，联合预测人体运动和肌肉激活，有效桥接了视觉观测与内部生物力学状态。大量实验表明，BioHuman能够准确重建运动学运动与肌肉活动，并在不同被试和动作类型上具有良好的泛化能力。我们相信，该方法为基于视频的生物力学理解建立了新基准，并为物理上可信的人体建模开辟了新可能。

</details>


### [67] [MonoPRIO: Adaptive Prior Conditioning for Unified Monocular 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14781)
*Leon Davies,Qinggang Meng,Mohamad Saada,Baihua Li,Simon Sølvsten*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MonoPRIO，一种通过自适应先验条件化（自适应先验条件化）来提升单目3D检测中尺寸估计稳定性的统一多类检测器，在KITTI上达到最优统一多类结果，并显著降低计算量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 单目3D检测中，度量尺寸和深度由单视图证据欠定（欠定），尤其在遮挡、截断和投影导致的尺度-深度模糊下。现有方法虽改进了深度和几何推理，但在统一多类设定下，由于类别变异性和部分可见性，尺寸估计仍不稳定。本文旨在解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 1) 离线构建类别感知的尺寸原型（class-aware size prototypes）；2) 每个解码器查询路由到一个软混合先验（soft mixture prior）；3) 采用不确定性感知的对数空间条件化（uncertainty-aware log-space conditioning）；4) 训练时对匹配的正样本使用Cluster-Aligned Prior (CAP)正则化。

Result: 在KITTI官方测试集上，MonoPRIO在报告完整Car/Pedestrian/Cyclist指标的已发表方法中取得最强统一多类结果；在仅检测Car的设置下，在Easy/Moderate/Hard三类上均达到最强3D边界框AP，且计算量远低于MonoCLUE。消融实验表明路由注入和CAP带来互补增益，在模糊、部分遮挡和低数据场景下收益最大。

Conclusion: 自适应先验在图像证据不足以确定度量尺寸时最为有效；但非典型几何或极端可见性损失仍可能导致路由先验与真实实例几何之间不匹配。

Original Abstract: Monocular 3D object detection remains challenging because metric size and depth are underdetermined by single-view evidence, particularly under occlusion, truncation, and projection-induced scale-depth ambiguity. Although recent methods improve depth and geometric reasoning, metric size remains unstable in unified multi-class settings, where class variability and partial visibility broaden plausible size modes. We propose MonoPRIO, a unified monocular 3D detector that targets this bottleneck through adaptive prior conditioning in the size pathway. MonoPRIO constructs class-aware size prototypes offline, routes each decoder query to a soft mixture prior, applies uncertainty-aware log-space conditioning, and uses Cluster-Aligned Prior (CAP) regularisation on matched positives during training. On the official KITTI test server, MonoPRIO achieves the strongest fully reported unified multi-class result among methods reporting complete Car, Pedestrian, and Cyclist metrics. In the car-only setting, it also achieves the strongest 3D bounding-box AP across Easy/Moderate/Hard categories among compared methods without extra data, while using substantially less compute than MonoCLUE. Ablations and diagnostics show complementary gains from routed injection and CAP, with the largest benefits in ambiguity-prone, partially occluded, and low-data regimes. These findings indicate that adaptive priors are most effective when image evidence underdetermines metric size, while atypical geometry or extreme visibility loss can still cause mismatch between routed priors and true instance geometry. Code, trained models, result logs, and reproducibility material are available at https://github.com/bigggs/MonoPRIO.

中文摘要翻译: 单目3D物体检测仍然具有挑战性，因为度量尺寸和深度由单视图证据欠定（underdetermined），尤其是在遮挡、截断和投影导致的尺度-深度模糊（scale-depth ambiguity）下。尽管近期方法改进了深度和几何推理，但在统一多类设定（unified multi-class setting）中，度量尺寸仍然不稳定，类别变异性和部分可见性拓宽了可能的尺寸模式。我们提出了MonoPRIO，一种统一的单目3D检测器，通过在尺寸通路（size pathway）中引入自适应先验条件化（adaptive prior conditioning）来针对这一瓶颈。MonoPRIO离线构建类别感知的尺寸原型（class-aware size prototypes），将每个解码器查询路由到一个软混合先验（soft mixture prior），应用不确定性感知的对数空间条件化（uncertainty-aware log-space conditioning），并在训练过程中对匹配的正样本使用聚类对齐先验（Cluster-Aligned Prior, CAP）正则化。在官方KITTI测试集上，MonoPRIO在报告完整Car、Pedestrian和Cyclist指标的已发表方法中取得了最强的统一多类结果。在仅检测Car的设置下，它在Easy/Moderate/Hard三个难度类别上也取得了相比不使用额外数据的对比方法中最强的3D边界框平均精度（AP），同时计算量远低于MonoCLUE。消融实验和诊断分析表明，路由注入（routed injection）和CAP具有互补的增益，在模糊易混淆、部分遮挡和低数据场景下收益最大。这些发现表明，当图像证据不足以确定度量尺寸时，自适应先验最为有效；但非典型几何或极端可见性损失仍可能导致路由先验与真实实例几何之间不匹配。代码、训练好的模型、结果日志和可复现性材料可在https://github.com/bigggs/MonoPRIO获取。

</details>


### [68] [Do Composed Image Retrieval Benchmarks Require Multimodal Composition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14787)
*Matteo Attimonelli,Alessandro De Bellis,Aryo Pradipta Gema,Rohit Saxena,Monica Sekoyan,Wai-Chung Kwan,Claudio Pomo,Alessandro Suglia,Dietmar Jannach,Tommaso Di Noia,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示组合图像检索（CIR）基准测试中存在大量单模态捷径（32.2%-83.6%），导致模型性能被高估；通过两阶段审计（跨模型分析+人工验证）构建了无捷径的子集，重新评估发现模型检索依赖真正的多模态组合，但准确率显著下降。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有CIR基准测试假定高性能需要多模态组合，但作者怀疑这一假设不成立，即模型可能依赖单模态捷径而非真正的多模态组合来解决查询，导致对模型多模态组合能力的过高估计。

Method: 两阶段审计：（1）通过跨模型分析识别出可通过单模态捷径解决的查询；（2）对4,741个无捷径查询进行人工验证，最终得到1,689个良构查询，在此子集上重新评估模型。

Result: 在四个CIR基准和十一个通用多模态嵌入模型上，32.2%-83.6%的查询可通过单模态解决；人工验证发现大量查询存在模糊编辑、目标不匹配等问题；在无捷径子集上，单模态无法解决问题，多模态信息依赖增加，但准确率下降。

Conclusion: 当前CIR基准测试混淆了捷径可解、噪声和真正组合性查询，导致对模型多模态组合能力的过高估计；需要更严谨的基准设计来评估真实的多模态组合能力。

Original Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) is a multimodal retrieval task where a query consists of a reference image and a textual modification, and the goal is to retrieve a target image satisfying both. In principle, strong performance on CIR benchmarks is assumed to require multimodal composition, i.e., combining complementary information from reference image and textual modification. In this work, we show that this assumption does not always hold. Across four widely used CIR benchmarks and eleven Generalist Multimodal Embedding models, a large fraction of queries can be solved using a single modality (from 32.2% to 83.6%), revealing pervasive unimodal shortcuts. Thus, high CIR performance can arise from unimodal signals rather than true multimodal composition. To better understand this issue, we perform a two-stage audit. First, we identify shortcut-solvable queries through cross-model analysis. Second, we conduct human validation on 4,741 shortcut-free queries, of which only 1,689 are well-formed, with common issues including ambiguous edits and mismatched targets. Re-evaluating models on this validated subset reveals qualitatively different behaviour: queries can no longer be solved with a single modality, and successful retrieval requires combining both inputs. While accuracy decreases, reliance on multimodal information increases. Overall, current CIR benchmarks conflate shortcut-solvable, noisy, and genuinely compositional queries, leading to an overestimation of model capability in multimodal composition.

中文摘要翻译: 组合图像检索（CIR）是一种多模态检索任务，其中查询由参考图像和文本修改组成，目标是检索满足两者的目标图像。原则上，CIR基准测试的高性能被认为需要多模态组合，即从参考图像和文本修改中结合互补信息。在这项工作中，我们表明这一假设并不总是成立。在四个广泛使用的CIR基准测试和十一个通用多模态嵌入模型上，大部分查询可以通过单一模态解决（从32.2%到83.6%），揭示出普遍存在的单模态捷径。因此，高CIR性能可能源于单模态信号而非真正的多模态组合。为了更好地理解这个问题，我们进行了两阶段审计。首先，通过跨模型分析识别出捷径可解的查询。其次，对4,741个无捷径查询进行人工验证，其中只有1,689个是良构的，常见问题包括模糊编辑和目标不匹配。在此验证子集上重新评估模型揭示出定性的不同行为：查询不再能通过单一模态解决，成功检索需要结合两个输入。虽然准确率下降，但对多模态信息的依赖增加。总体而言，当前CIR基准测试混淆了捷径可解、噪声和真正组合性查询，导致对模型多模态组合能力的过高估计。

</details>


### [69] [COAL: Counterfactual and Observation-Enhanced Alignment Learning for Discriminative Referring Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14795)
*Shukun Jia,Shiyu Hu,Yipei Wang,Ximeng Cheng,Yichao Cao,Xiaobo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COAL框架通过知识正则化（包括VLM显式语义注入和LLM反事实学习）解决RMOT中高判别需求与稀疏语义监督之间的结构性矛盾，在Refer-KITTI-V2上超越SOTA达7.28% HOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: RMOT面临高判别需求与稀疏语义监督之间的结构性矛盾，尤其在高度同质化场景中，模型在稀疏监督下容易过拟合到显著但不充分的线索，导致捷径学习和语义崩塌。

Method: 提出COAL框架：1) 通过VLM进行显式语义注入(ESI)以稠密化观测空间；2) 利用LLM推理提出反事实学习(CFL)，通过严格属性验证增强组合识别；3) 设计层次化多流集成(HMSI)架构将外部知识蒸馏为领域特定的判别性表示。

Result: 在Refer-KITTI和Refer-KITTI-V2基准上验证了COAL的有效性，在极具挑战性的Refer-KITTI-V2上以7.28% HOTA超越当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 知识正则化有效解决了RMOT中的稀疏性与判别性悖论，证明了外部知识注入对于提升多目标跟踪中细粒度语义判别能力的关键作用。

Original Abstract: Referring Multi-Object Tracking (RMOT) faces a fundamental structural contradiction between the high-discriminability demand and the sparse semantic supervision. This mismatch is particularly acute in highly homogeneous scenarios that require fine-grained discrimination over complex compositional semantics. However, under sparse supervision, models overfit to salient yet insufficient cues, thereby encouraging shortcut learning and semantic collapse. To resolve this, we propose COAL (Counterfactual and Observation-enhanced Alignment Learning), a framework that advances RMOT beyond isolated structural optimization through knowledge regularization. First, we introduce Explicit Semantic Injection (ESI) via a VLM to densify the observation space and enhance instance discriminability. Second, leveraging LLM reasoning, we propose Counterfactual Learning (CFL) to augment supervision, enforcing strict attribute verification for robust compositional recognition. These strategies are unified within a Hierarchical Multi-Stream Integration (HMSI) architecture, which distills external knowledge into domain-specific discriminative representations. Experiments on Refer-KITTI and Refer-KITTI-V2 benchmarks validate COAL's efficacy. Notably, it surpasses the state-of-the-art by 7.28% HOTA on the highly challenging Refer-KITTI-V2. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of knowledge regularization for resolving the sparsity-discriminability paradox in RMOT.

中文摘要翻译: 引用多目标跟踪（RMOT）面临高判别需求与稀疏语义监督之间的根本性结构矛盾。这种不匹配在需要基于复杂组合语义进行细粒度区分的同质化场景中尤为突出。然而，在稀疏监督下，模型会过拟合到显著但不充分的线索，从而鼓励捷径学习和语义崩塌。为解决此问题，我们提出COAL（反事实与观测增强对齐学习）框架，该框架通过知识正则化推动RMOT超越孤立的结构优化。首先，我们通过VLM引入显式语义注入（ESI）以稠密化观测空间并增强实例判别性。其次，利用LLM推理，我们提出反事实学习（CFL）来增强监督，通过严格属性验证实现鲁棒的组合识别。这些策略统一在层次化多流集成（HMSI）架构中，将外部知识蒸馏为领域特定的判别性表示。在Refer-KITTI和Refer-KITTI-V2基准上的实验验证了COAL的有效性。值得注意的是，它在极具挑战性的Refer-KITTI-V2上以7.28% HOTA超越了当前最先进方法。这些结果证明了知识正则化在解决RMOT中稀疏性与判别性悖论方面的有效性。

</details>


### [70] [Probing into Camera Control of Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14815)
*Chen Hou,Christian Rupprecht*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将相机控制重写为位移场并通过可微重采样作用于去噪过程，无需训练即可实现视频扩散模型的相机控制，并作为探针揭示基础模型的相机控制偏差与多视角生成能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法需学习从相机运动到视频的隐式映射，依赖额外相机模块和配对数据，但此类数据集规模有限、多样性不足，会限制模型的输出分布并损害基础模型的先验知识，因此需要一种更简洁、无需训练的相机控制方法。

Method: 将相机控制重新表述为一组位移场（displacement fields），在去噪过程中通过可微重采样（differentiable resampling）对潜在特征进行变换，从而实现对视频扩散模型的相机控制，无需额外训练。

Result: 该方法与微调基线相比，在多种质量指标上退化极小；可作为探针揭示代表性视频模型普遍存在的相机控制偏差以及它们对相机控制响应的差异；并在多视角生成任务上对模型进行了基准测试。

Conclusion: 相机控制可以不必建模为隐式映射问题，而视为一种几何引导的位移场形式，该方法无需训练即可实现有效的相机控制，同时为研究基础模型的相机控制能力提供了分析工具。

Original Abstract: Video is a rich and scalable source of 3D/4D visual observations, and camera control is a key capability for video generation models to produce geometrically meaningful content. Existing approaches typically learn a mapping from camera motion to video using additional camera modules and paired data. However, such datasets are often limited in scale, diversity, and scene dynamics, which can bias the model toward a narrow output distribution and compromise the strong prior learned by the base model. These limitations motivate a different perspective on camera control. In this paper, we show that camera control need not be modeled as an implicit mapping problem, but can instead be treated as a form of geometric guidance that induces displacements across frames. Specifically, we reformulate camera control into a set of displacement fields and apply them via differentiable resampling of latent features during denoising. Our simple approach achieves effective camera control with minimal degradation across diverse quality metrics compared to fine-tuned baselines. Since our method is applicable to most video diffusion models without training, it can also serve as a probe to study the camera control capabilities of base models. Using this probe, we identify universal biases shared by representative video models, as well as disparities in their responses to camera control. Finally, we benchmark their performance in multi-view generation, offering insights into their potential for 3D/4D tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 视频是丰富的、可扩展的3D/4D视觉观测来源，而相机控制是视频生成模型生成具有几何意义的内容的关键能力。现有方法通常通过额外的相机模块和配对数据来学习从相机运动到视频的映射。然而，此类数据集通常在规模、多样性和场景动态方面存在局限，这会使模型偏向于狭窄的输出分布，并损害基础模型学习到的强先验。这些局限性促使我们从一个不同的视角看待相机控制。本文表明，相机控制不必建模为隐式映射问题，而是可以将其视为一种几何引导形式，在帧间诱导位移。具体地，我们将相机控制重新表述为一组位移场，并在去噪过程中通过可微重采样对潜在特征进行变换。与微调基线相比，我们的简单方法在多种质量指标上实现了有效的相机控制且退化极小。由于该方法无需训练即可适用于大多数视频扩散模型，它也可以作为探针来研究基础模型的相机控制能力。利用该探针，我们识别了代表性视频模型普遍存在的共同偏差，以及它们在相机控制响应上的差异。最后，我们对其在多视角生成中的表现进行了基准测试，为它们在3D/4D任务中的潜力提供了见解。

</details>


### [71] [The Velocity Deficit: Initial Energy Injection for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14819)
*Linze Li,Zong-Wei Hong,Shen Zhang,Bo Lin,Jinglun Li,Yao Tang,Jiajun Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现流匹配在高维场景中存在“速度赤字”问题，即MSE损失低估速度大小导致生成样本无法到达数据流形。提出初始能量注入方法（MAFM和SSC），通过补偿起始阶段的速度来缓解积分滞后，在不增加训练成本的情况下大幅提升生成质量和效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 流匹配理论上保证恒定速度轨迹，但实践中高维空间下MSE目标函数会系统性地低估速度大小，导致生成样本无法到达数据流形（积分滞后现象）。作者希望解决这一根本性缺陷。

Method: 提出初始能量注入（Initial Energy Injection），包含两种互补方法：（1）基于训练的幅度感知流匹配（MAFM）；（2）无需训练的尺度调度校正器（SSC），仅需一行代码且无需重新训练。两者基于速度收缩的不对称性：轨迹起始处造成有害动能停滞，末端则是有益的去噪机制。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k（256×256）上，SSC将FID从13.68提升至7.58（改进44.6%），实现5倍加速；50步生成器（FID 7.58）超越250步基线（FID 8.65）。在MS-COCO文生图任务上FID改进约22%，且方法可泛化至高分辨率生成。

Conclusion: 速度赤字是流匹配在高维实践中的关键瓶颈，初始能量注入能有效补偿速度低估，显著提升生成质量和采样效率。SSC方法简单高效，无需重新训练即可实现大幅性能提升，具有良好的泛化性。

Original Abstract: While Flow Matching theoretically guarantees constant-velocity trajectories, we identify a critical breakdown in high-dimensional practice: the Velocity Deficit. We show that the MSE objective systematically underestimates velocity magnitude, causing generated samples to fail to reach the data manifold-a phenomenon we term Integration Lag. To rectify this, we propose Initial Energy Injection, instantiated via two complementary methods: the training-based Magnitude-Aware Flow Matching (MAFM) and the training-free Scale Schedule Corrector (SSC). Both are grounded in our discovery of a crucial asymmetry: velocity contraction causes harmful kinetic stagnation at the trajectory's start, yet acts as a beneficial denoising mechanism at its end. Empirically, SSC yields significant efficiency gains with zero retraining and just one line of code. On ImageNet-1k (256x256), it improves FID by 44.6% (from 13.68 to 7.58) and achieves a 5x speedup, enabling a 50-step generator (FID 7.58) to beat a 250-step baseline (FID 8.65). Furthermore, our methods generalize to Text-to-Image tasks and high-resolution generation, improving FID on MS-COCO by ~22%.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然流匹配（Flow Matching）在理论上保证了恒定速度的轨迹，但我们发现在高维实践中存在一个关键缺陷：速度赤字（Velocity Deficit）。我们发现，MSE目标函数会系统性地低估速度大小，导致生成的样本无法到达数据流形——我们将这种现象称为积分滞后（Integration Lag）。为纠正这一问题，我们提出了初始能量注入（Initial Energy Injection），通过两种互补方法实现：基于训练的幅度感知流匹配（MAFM）和无需训练的尺度调度校正器（SSC）。两者均基于我们的关键发现：速度收缩在轨迹起始阶段会造成有害的动能停滞，但在轨迹末端却起着有益的去噪作用。实验表明，SSC无需重新训练且仅需一行代码即可实现显著的效率提升。在ImageNet-1k（256x256）上，它将FID从13.68提升至7.58（改进44.6%），实现5倍加速，使得50步生成器（FID 7.58）超越了250步基线（FID 8.65）。此外，我们的方法可泛化至文生图任务和高分辨率生成，在MS-COCO上将FID改进了约22%。

</details>


### [72] [Editor's Choice: Evaluating Abstract Intent in Image Editing through Atomic Entity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14842)
*Mor Ventura,Roy Hirsch,Yonatan Bitton,Regev Cohen,Roi Reichart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了抽象图像编辑任务，构建了Entity-Rubrics评估框架和AbstractEdit基准数据集，发现现有模型在抽象指令下存在欠编辑或过编辑问题，并指出集成先进LLM文本编码器与迭代推理是提升性能的关键。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像编辑基准主要关注显式、字面指令，而人类自然交流中常使用抽象概念（如“情绪”），抽象指令下的图像编辑能力尚未被系统探索和评估。

Method: 形式化定义了抽象图像编辑的分类体系；提出Entity-Rubrics框架，将抽象编辑分解为逐实体评估，与人类判断高度相关；构建了覆盖多样真实场景的AbstractEdit基准数据集。

Result: 评估11个领先模型发现：标准架构难以平衡意图保持与内容保留，普遍存在欠编辑或过编辑问题；集成先进LLM文本编码器和迭代推理机制是提升性能的关键。

Conclusion: 基于实体的评估范式可泛化至奖励模型、辅助模型理解抽象交流、作为测试时纠错工具，推动多模态交互从刚性机器执行向人类自然交流方式迈进。

Original Abstract: Humans naturally communicate through abstract concepts like "mood". However, current image editing benchmarks focus primarily on explicit, literal commands, leaving abstract instructions largely underexplored. In this work, we first formalize the definition and taxonomy of abstract image editing. To measure instruction-following in this challenging domain, we introduce Entity-Rubrics, a framework that breaks down abstract edits into individual, entity-level assessments and achieves strong correlation with human judgment. Alongside this framework, we contribute AbstractEdit, the first benchmark dedicated to abstract image editing across diverse real-world scenes. Evaluating 11 leading models on this dataset reveals a fundamental challenge: standard architectures struggle to balance intent and preservation, commonly defaulting to under-editing or over-editing. Our analysis demonstrates that driving meaningful improvements relies heavily on integrating advanced LLM text encoders and iterative thinking. Looking forward, our entity-based paradigm can generalize beyond assessment to serve as a reward model, enable models to correctly interpret abstract communication, or highlight specific failures in test-time critique loops. Ultimately, we hope this work serves as a stepping stone toward seamless multimodal interaction, closing the gap between rigid machine execution and the natural, open-ended way humans communicate.

中文摘要翻译: 人类自然交流中常使用如“情绪”这样的抽象概念。然而，当前的图像编辑基准主要关注显式、字面的指令，抽象指令在很大程度上仍未得到探索。本文首先形式化定义了抽象图像编辑的概念与分类体系。为衡量这一挑战性领域中的指令遵循能力，我们提出了Entity-Rubrics框架，该框架将抽象编辑分解为逐实体级别的评估，并与人类判断实现了强相关性。伴随该框架，我们贡献了AbstractEdit——首个专注于抽象图像编辑的基准数据集，覆盖多种真实场景。对11个领先模型在该数据集上的评估揭示了一个根本性挑战：标准架构难以平衡意图保持与内容保留，普遍倾向于欠编辑或过编辑。我们的分析表明，推动有意义的改进在很大程度上依赖于集成先进的LLM文本编码器与迭代推理机制。展望未来，我们提出的基于实体的评估范式可泛化至评估之外，作为奖励模型、帮助模型正确解读抽象交流，或在测试时纠错循环中突出特定失败案例。最终，我们希望这项工作能够成为通向无缝多模态交互的垫脚石，缩小刚性机器执行与人类自然、开放式交流方式之间的差距。

</details>


### [73] [MechVerse: Evaluating Physical Motion Consistency in Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14843)
*Rahul Jain,Mayank Patel,Asim Unmesh,Karthik Ramani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MechVerse 基准，用于评估图像到视频生成模型在机械一致性方面的表现，发现现有模型在视觉保真度上表现良好，但在生成符合运动学和几何约束的机械运动方面存在严重缺陷，且错误率随耦合复杂度增加而上升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本和图像条件视频生成模型虽在视觉保真度和时间一致性上表现优异，但常生成违反运动学和几何约束的运动（如应该平移的部件被旋转、刚性部件变形、部件间耦合断裂等），尤其在具有明确机械约束的装配体场景中问题突出。目前缺乏系统性基准来评估视频生成模型对机械运动一致性的理解能力。

Method: 构建 MechVerse 基准，包含来自141个类别、1357个机械装配体的21,156个合成视频片段，按运动学复杂度分为三个层级：独立关节运动、成对耦合运动、密集耦合多部件机构。每个视频配有结构化提示，描述部件身份、固定支撑、运动部件、运动基元、方向、速度/幅度及部件间依赖关系。使用标准视频指标、指令跟随分数和人工判断对多种视频生成模型进行评估。

Result: 现有模型（包括闭源、开源和微调模型）在保持外观和运动平滑性方面表现良好，但未能生成机械上可接受的运动；错误率随耦合复杂度增加而上升。

Conclusion: MechVerse 为从图像和语言输入出发的、考虑机械机制的视频生成提供了评测基准，可帮助衡量和提升模型对运动学约束的理解与生成能力。

Original Abstract: Text- and image-conditioned video generation models have achieved strong visual fidelity and temporal coherence, but they often fail to generate motion governed by kinematic and geometric constraints. In these settings, object parts must remain rigid, maintain contact or coupling with neighboring components, and transfer motion consistently across connected parts. These requirements are especially explicit in articulated mechanical assemblies, where motion is constrained by rigid-link geometry, contact/coupling relations, and transmission through kinematic chains. A generated video may therefore appear plausible while violating the intended mechanism, such as rotating a part that should translate, deforming a rigid component, breaking coupling between parts, or failing to move downstream components. To evaluate this gap, We introduce MechVerse, a benchmark for mechanically consistent image-to-video generation. MechVerse contains 21,156 synthetic clips from 1,357 mechanical assemblies across 141 categories, organized into three tiers of increasing kinematic complexity: independent articulation, pairwise coupling, and densely coupled multi-part mechanisms. Each clip is paired with a structured prompt describing part identities, stationary supports, moving components, motion primitives, direction, speed/extent, and inter-part dependencies. We evaluate proprietary, open-source, and fine-tuned image-to-video models using standard video metrics, instruction-following scores, and human judgments of motion correctness and kinematic coupling. Results show that current models can preserve appearance and smoothness while failing to generate mechanically admissible motion, with errors increasing as coupling complexity grows. MechVerse provides a benchmark for measuring and improving mechanism-aware video generation from image and language inputs.

中文摘要翻译: 文本和图像条件视频生成模型在视觉保真度和时间一致性方面取得了显著成果，但它们往往无法生成受运动学和几何约束支配的运动。在这些场景中，物体部件必须保持刚性，与相邻组件保持接触或耦合关系，并在连接的部件之间一致地传递运动。这些要求在铰接式机械装配体中尤为明确——其运动受刚性连杆几何结构、接触/耦合关系以及通过运动链的传动约束。因此，生成的视频可能看似合理，却违反了预期的机构原理，例如旋转本应平移的部件、使刚性部件变形、破坏部件之间的耦合，或未能驱动下游部件运动。为评估这一差距，我们提出了 MechVerse——一个面向机械一致性图像到视频生成的基准。MechVerse 包含来自141个类别、1357个机械装配体的21,156个合成视频片段，按照运动学复杂度的递增分为三个层级：独立关节运动、成对耦合运动以及密集耦合的多部件机构。每个视频片段配有结构化提示，描述部件身份、固定支撑、运动部件、运动基元、方向、速度/幅度以及部件间依赖关系。我们使用标准视频指标、指令跟随分数以及基于人工判断的运动正确性和运动学耦合评分，对闭源、开源和微调后的图像到视频模型进行评估。结果表明，当前模型能够保持外观和运动平滑性，但无法生成机械上可接受的运动，且错误率随耦合复杂度的增加而上升。MechVolume 提供了一个从图像和语言输入出发、衡量和改进机构感知视频生成的基准。

</details>


### [74] [Exploring Vision-Language Models for Online Signature Verification: A Zero-Shot Capability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14845)
*Marta Robledo-Moreno,Ruben Vera-Rodriguez,Ruben Tolosana,Javier Ortega-Garcia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 探索性研究评估了GPT-5.2和Gemini 2.5 Pro等VLM在签名验证任务上的零样本表现，发现随机伪造场景下性能优越（EER低至0.32%），但熟练伪造场景下出现"合理化陷阱"：链式思维推理反而因产生运动幻觉而降低性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管VLM在通用视觉推理中表现强劲，但其在严谨的生物特征识别任务（如签名验证）中的适用性尚未被探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将原始动力学时间序列转换为静态图像（编码压力信息为笔画透明度）；并设计评分协议，通过提取潜在token概率来计算生物特征分数，实现零样本签名验证。

Result: 随机伪造场景下GPT-5.2在移动任务中EER仅0.32%，超越监督式SOTA；熟练伪造场景下性能显著下降，链式思维推理引发"合理化陷阱"，模型产生运动学幻觉将伪造痕迹错误解释为自然变异。

Conclusion: VLM在签名验证任务上展现出潜力与局限：能轻松区分随机伪造，但在面对视觉上几乎相同的熟练伪造时缺乏判别能力，且链式思维推理在此场景下有害。

Original Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in general visual reasoning, yet their applicability to rigorous biometric tasks remains unexplored. This work presents an exploratory study evaluating the zero-shot performance of state-of-the-art VLMs (GPT-5.2 and Gemini 2.5 Pro) on the Signature Verification Challenge (SVC) benchmark. To enable visual processing, raw kinematic time-series are converted into static images, encoding pressure information into stroke opacity whenever available in the source data. Furthermore, we introduce a scoring protocol that extracts latent token probabilities to compute robust biometric scores. Experimental results reveal a significant performance dichotomy dependent on signal quality and forgery type. In random forgery scenarios, the zero-shot VLM achieves exceptional discrimination, with GPT-5.2 reaching an Equal Error Rate of 0.32% in mobile tasks, outperforming supervised state-of-the-art systems. Conversely, in skilled forgery scenarios, where the task is more challenging because both signatures are almost identical, the results are significantly worse, and a critical "Rationalization Trap" emerges: chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning degrades performance as the model produces kinematic hallucinations to justify forgery artifacts as natural variability.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）的最新进展已展现出强大的通用视觉推理能力，但其在严谨的生物特征识别任务中的适用性尚未被探索。本文提出了一项探索性研究，评估了最先进的VLM（GPT-5.2和Gemini 2.5 Pro）在签名验证挑战（SVC）基准上的零样本性能。为了实现视觉处理，我们将原始动力学时间序列转换为静态图像，并在源数据可用时将压力信息编码为笔画透明度。此外，我们引入了一种评分协议，通过提取潜在token概率来计算鲁棒的生物特征分数。实验结果显示，性能存在显著的二分性，取决于信号质量和伪造类型。在随机伪造场景下，零样本VLM实现了卓越的区分能力，GPT-5.2在移动任务中达到了0.32%的等错误率（EER），超越了监督式最先进系统。相反，在熟练伪造场景下——由于两个签名几乎完全相同，任务更具挑战性——结果显著变差，并出现了一个关键的"合理化陷阱"：链式思维（CoT）推理会降低性能，因为模型会产生运动学幻觉，将伪造痕迹解释为自然变异。

</details>


### [75] [SR-Prominence: A Crowdsourced Protocol and Dataset Suite for Perceptually-Weighted Super-Resolution Artifact Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14847)
*Ivan Molodetskikh,Kirill Malyshev,Mark Mirgaleev,Nikita Zagainov,Evgeney Bogatyrev,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出以“伪影显著性”作为超分辨率图像质量评估的新目标，通过众包标注构建了包含3935个伪影掩码的SR-Prominence数据集，发现现有数据集中的二元伪影标签有48.2%不被多数观察者注意，并验证了经典全参考指标在局部显著性评估上的优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有超分辨率方法生成的图像存在视觉伪影，且不同伪影的感知影响差异巨大（有些几乎不可见，有些非常令人不安），但现有检测方法对所有伪影一视同仁，缺乏基于感知影响的差异化评估。

Method: 定义“伪影显著性”为认为高亮区域包含明显伪影的观看者比例；设计众包标注协议；构建SR-Prominence数据集套件（包含DeSRA、Open Images、Urban100和Urban100-HR四个子集，共3935个伪影掩码并标注显著性分数）；提供客观评分协议使新指标无需额外众包即可在该套件上评测。

Result: 重新标注DeSRA发现其48.2%的二元伪影不被多数观看者注意；经典全参考指标（特别是SSIM和DISTS）在局部显著性评估上表现优异，而无参考IQA方法和专用伪影检测器在跨数据集和参考设置下泛化能力不足。

Conclusion: SR伪影评估应从二元缺陷存在性转向感知影响评估；所提出的数据集和协议为这一转变提供了标准化基准和客观评测手段。

Original Abstract: Modern image super-resolution methods generate detailed, visually appealing results, but they often introduce visual artifacts: unnatural patterns and texture distortions that degrade perceived quality. These defects vary widely in perceptual impact--some are barely noticeable, while others are highly disturbing--yet existing detection methods treat them equally. We propose artifact prominence as an evaluative target, defined as the fraction of viewers who judge a highlighted region to contain a noticeable artifact. We design a crowdsourced annotation protocol and construct SR-Prominence, a dataset suite containing 3,935 artifact masks from DeSRA, Open Images, Urban100, and a realistic no-ground-truth Urban100-HR setting, annotated with prominence. Re-annotating DeSRA reveals that 48.2% of its in-lab binary artifacts are not noticed by a majority of viewers. Across the suite, we audit SR artifact detectors, image-quality metrics, and SR methods. We find that classical full-reference metrics, especially SSIM and DISTS, provide surprisingly strong localized prominence signals, whereas no-reference IQA methods and specialized artifact detectors often fail to generalize across datasets and reference settings. SR-Prominence is released with an objective scoring protocol that allows new metrics to be benchmarked on our suite without further crowdsourcing. Together, the data and protocols enable SR artifact evaluation to move from binary defect presence toward perceptual impact. SR-Prominence is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/imolodetskikh/sr-artifact-prominence.

中文摘要翻译: 现代图像超分辨率方法能生成细节丰富、视觉吸引人的结果，但常常引入视觉伪影——不自然的图案和纹理失真，降低了感知质量。这些缺陷在感知影响上差异巨大——有些几乎不可察觉，而另一些则非常令人不安——然而现有的检测方法对它们一视同仁。我们提出将伪影显著性作为评估目标，定义为认为高亮区域包含明显伪影的观看者比例。我们设计了一种众包标注协议，并构建了SR-Prominence数据集套件，包含来自DeSRA、Open Images、Urban100以及一个无真实参考的Urban100-HR真实场景的3935个伪影掩码，均标注了显著性分数。重新标注DeSRA发现，其48.2%的实验室二元伪影不被多数观看者注意到。在整个套件上，我们对SR伪影检测器、图像质量指标和SR方法进行了审计。我们发现经典的全参考指标，特别是SSIM和DISTS，在局部显著性信号上表现出令人惊讶的强相关性，而无参考IQA方法和专用伪影检测器往往难以跨数据集和参考设置进行泛化。SR-Prominence发布了一个客观评分协议，允许新指标无需额外的众包即可在我们的套件上进行基准测试。这些数据和协议共同使SR伪影评估从二元缺陷存在性转向感知影响。SR-Prominence可在https://huggingface.co/datasets/imolodetskikh/sr-artifact-prominence获取。

</details>


### [76] [FactorizedHMR: A Hybrid Framework for Video Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14854)
*Patrick Kwon,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FactorizedHMR提出一种两阶段框架，将人体网格重建中的确定性躯干回归与概率性流匹配完成分离，以处理姿态模糊性，在遮挡和世界坐标系指标上表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 人体网格重建存在本质歧义性：在遮挡或弱深度线索下，同一图像证据可对应多个3D身体姿态，且这种歧义在身体各部位分布不均——躯干和根部结构相对确定，而四肢等远端关节不确定性更高。现有方法未对此差异化处理。

Method: 提出两阶段框架FactorizedHMR：第一阶段用确定性回归模块恢复稳定的躯干-根部锚点；第二阶段用概率性流匹配模块完成剩余的非躯干关节姿态。为提升完成可靠性，结合了复合目标表示、几何感知监督和特征感知无分类器引导，在保留躯干-根部锚点的同时改进歧义关节的单参考恢复。还引入合成数据管道提供成对的图像-相机-运动监督信号。

Result: 在相机空间和世界空间基准上，FactorizedHMR与强基线方法竞争力相当，在严重遮挡恢复和易漂移的世界空间指标上提升最为明显。

Conclusion: 通过区分处理躯干和四肢的不确定性，两阶段分解策略能有效处理HMR中的姿态歧义，尤其在遮挡场景和世界坐标系下性能突出。

Original Abstract: Human Mesh Recovery (HMR) is fundamentally ambiguous: under occlusion or weak depth cues, multiple 3D bodies can explain the same image evidence. This ambiguity is not uniform across the body, as torso pose and root structure are often relatively well constrained, whereas distal articulations such as the arms and legs are more uncertain. Building on this observation, we propose FactorizedHMR, a two-stage framework that treats these two regimes differently. A deterministic regression module first recovers a stable torso-root anchor, and a probabilistic flow-matching module then completes the remaining non-torso articulation. To make this completion reliable, we combine a composite target representation with geometry-aware supervision and feature-aware classifier-free guidance, preserving the torso-root anchor while improving single-reference recovery of ambiguity-prone articulation. We also introduce a synthetic data pipeline that provides the paired image-camera-motion supervision under diverse viewpoints. Across camera-space and world-space benchmarks, FactorizedHMR remains competitive with strong baselines, with the clearest gains in occlusion-heavy recovery and drift-sensitive world-space metrics.

中文摘要翻译: 人体网格重建（HMR）在本质上是歧义的：在遮挡或弱深度线索下，相同的图像证据可以对应多个3D人体姿态。这种歧义性在身体各部位并非均匀分布——躯干姿态和根部结构通常相对明确，而手臂和腿部等远端关节则更具不确定性。基于这一观察，我们提出了FactorizedHMR，一种将这两个区域区别对待的两阶段框架。确定性回归模块首先恢复稳定的躯干-根部锚点，然后概率性流匹配模块完成剩余的非躯干关节姿态。为了使这一完成过程可靠，我们结合了复合目标表示、几何感知监督和特征感知无分类器引导，在保留躯干-根部锚点的同时改进了歧义关节的单参考恢复。我们还引入了一个合成数据管道，在多样化的视角下提供配对的图像-相机-运动监督信号。在相机空间和世界空间基准测试中，FactorizedHMR与强基线方法相比保持竞争力，在严重遮挡恢复和对漂移敏感的世界空间指标上取得了最显著的提升。

</details>


### [77] [LPH-VTON: Resolving the Structure-Texture Dilemma of Virtual Try-On via Latent Process Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14874)
*Yixin Liu,Baihong Qian,Jinglin Jiang,Jeffery Wu,Yan Chen,Wei Wang,Yida Wang,Lanqing Yang,Guangtao Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LPH-VTON框架，通过在单一连续去噪过程中先利用结构偏置模型建立几何一致的潜在支架，再交由纹理偏置模型进行高保真细节渲染，解决了虚拟试衣中结构完整性与纹理保真度之间的根本性权衡问题，在VITON-HD数据集上取得了新的最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前基于扩散的虚拟试衣方法在结构完整性和纹理保真度之间存在根本性权衡：依赖空间约束的模型倾向于几何对齐但常抑制纹理，而受无约束生成先验主导的模型擅长细节渲染但易发生结构漂移。

Method: 提出LPH-VTON协同框架，在单一连续去噪过程中策略性地分解生成过程：早期阶段利用结构偏置模型建立几何一致的潜在支架，后期阶段切换至纹理偏置模型进行高保真细节渲染，实现时间维度的架构解耦。

Result: 在VITON-HD数据集上，模型实现了帕累托最优平衡，在感知忠实度上建立了新基准，同时保持了极具竞争力的结构对齐性能。

Conclusion: 时间架构解耦策略能够有效解决虚拟试衣中结构完整性与纹理保真度之间的权衡问题，证明了该方法的有效性。

Original Abstract: Virtual Try-On (VTON) aims to synthesize photorealistic images of garments precisely aligned with a person's body and pose. Current diffusion-based methods, however, face a fundamental trade-off between structural integrity and textural fidelity. In this paper, we formalize this challenge as a consequence of complementary inductive biases inherent in prevailing architectures: models heavily reliant on spatial constraints naturally favor geometric alignment but often suppress textures, whereas models dominated by unconstrained generative priors excel at vibrant detail rendering but are prone to structural drift. Based on this diagnosis, we propose LPH-VTON, a new synergistic framework that resolves this tension within a single, continuous denoising process. LPH-VTON strategically decomposes the generation, leveraging a structure-biased model to establish a geometrically consistent latent scaffold in the early stages, before handing over control to a texture-biased model for high-fidelity detail rendering. Extensive experiments validate our approach. Our model achieves a superior Pareto-optimal balance, establishing new benchmarks in perceptual faithfulness while maintaining highly competitive structural alignment across the standard dataset VITON-HD, proving the efficacy of temporal architectural decoupling.

中文摘要翻译: 虚拟试衣（VTON）旨在合成与人体姿态和姿势精确对齐的逼真服装图像。然而，当前基于扩散的方法面临着结构完整性与纹理保真度之间的根本性权衡。本文将此挑战形式化为现有架构中固有互补归纳偏置的后果：严重依赖空间约束的模型自然倾向于几何对齐，但往往会抑制纹理；而以无约束生成先验主导的模型擅长生动细节渲染，但容易发生结构漂移。基于这一诊断，我们提出LPH-VTON，一种新的协同框架，在单一连续去噪过程中解决了这一矛盾。LPH-VTON策略性地分解生成过程，在早期阶段利用结构偏置模型建立几何一致的潜在支架，随后将控制权移交给纹理偏置模型以进行高保真细节渲染。大量实验验证了我们的方法。我们的模型实现了优越的帕累托最优平衡，在标准数据集VITON-HD上建立了感知忠实度的新基准，同时保持了极具竞争力的结构对齐性能，证明了时间架构解耦的有效性。

</details>


### [78] [HeatKV: Head-tuned KV-cache Compression for Visual Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14877)
*Jonathan Cederlund,Axel Berg,Durmus Alp Emre Acar,Chuteng Zhou,Pontus Giselsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeatKV是一种针对视觉自回归(VAR)模型的高效KV缓存压缩方法，通过基于注意力分数对每个注意力头进行排序并构建静态剪枝调度，在Infinity-2B模型上实现了2倍于现有方法的压缩比，同时保持或提升图像质量，达到了VAR模型KV缓存压缩的新SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: VAR模型在图像生成中虽然质量高、延迟低，但存在严重的KV缓存内存瓶颈，每张生成图像需要数GB内存，亟需有效的压缩方法。

Method: HeatKV提出一种基于注意力头粒度的自适应缓存分配方法：首先利用小规模离线校准集，根据每个注意力头对先前生成尺度的注意力分数对其进行排序；然后基于该排序，在给定内存预算下构建静态剪枝调度，实现细粒度的、按头特定的缓存分配。

Result: 在Infinity-2B模型上，HeatKV相比现有方法实现了2倍更高的KV缓存压缩比，同时保持或提升了图像保真度、提示对齐度和人类感知评分，达到了VAR模型KV缓存压缩的新SOTA。

Conclusion: HeatKV验证了细粒度、按头特定的缓存分配策略在VAR模型KV缓存压缩中的有效性，为缓解VAR模型推理时的内存瓶颈提供了新的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Visual Autoregressive (VAR) models have recently demonstrated impressive image generation quality while maintaining low latency. However, they suffer from severe KV-cache memory constraints, often requiring gigabytes of memory per generated image. We introduce HeatKV, a novel compression method that adapts cache allocation in each head based on its attention to previously generated scales. Using a small offline calibration set, the attention heads are ranked according to their attention scores over prior scales. Based on this ranking, we construct a static pruning schedule tailored to a given memory budget. Applied to the Infinity-2B model, HeatKV achieves $2 \times$ higher compression ratio in memory allocation for KV cache compared to existing methods, while maintaining similar or better image fidelity, prompt alignment and human perception score. Our method achieves a new state-of-the-art (SOTA) for VAR model KV-cache compression, showcasing the effectiveness of fine-grained, head-specific cache allocation.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉自回归（VAR）模型最近在保持低延迟的同时展现了令人印象深刻的图像生成质量。然而，它们面临着严重的KV缓存内存限制，每生成一张图像通常需要数GB的内存。我们提出了HeatKV，一种新颖的压缩方法，它根据每个注意力头对先前生成尺度的注意力来自适应地分配缓存。通过使用一个小型离线校准集，注意力头根据它们对先前尺度的注意力分数进行排序。基于这个排序，我们在给定的内存预算下构建了一个静态剪枝调度。将其应用于Infinity-2B模型，HeatKV在KV缓存的内存分配上实现了比现有方法高2倍的压缩比，同时保持或更优的图像保真度、提示对齐度和人类感知评分。我们的方法实现了VAR模型KV缓存压缩的新最优结果（SOTA），展示了细粒度、按头特定缓存分配的有效性。

</details>


### [79] [Denoising-GS: Gaussian Splatting with Spatial-aware Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14880)
*Qingyuan Zhou,Xinyi Liu,Weidong Yang,Ning Wang,Shuquan Ye,Ben Fei,Ying He,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Denoising-GS，将3D高斯溅射优化视为原始素去噪过程，通过空间感知去噪框架（包括空间梯度去噪、不确定性去噪和空间一致性精炼）来减少噪声原始素，提升新视角合成质量并保持紧凑性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3DGS优化过程因SfM点云的稀疏和不完全初始化而不可避免地引入噪声高斯原始素，且大多数方法仅关注优化中调整原始素位置而忽略其底层空间结构。

Method: 设计了一个保持原始素空间优化流的优化器；提出空间梯度去噪策略联合考虑原始素的空间支持以实现梯度一致性更新；设计不确定性去噪模块估计原始素级别的不确定性以修剪冗余/噪声原始素；通过空间一致性精炼策略在稀疏区域选择性分裂原始素以保持结构完整性。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，Denoising-GS一致提升了NVS保真度并保持了表示紧凑性，在所有基准上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过将3DGS优化形式化为原始素去噪过程并引入空间感知去噪框架，能够有效减少噪声原始素，提升新视角合成质量，同时保持模型紧凑性。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have achieved remarkable success in high-fidelity Novel View Synthesis (NVS), yet the optimization process inevitably introduces noisy Gaussian primitives due to the sparse and incomplete initialization from Structure-from-Motion (SfM) point clouds. Most existing methods focus solely on adjusting the positions of primitives during optimization, while neglecting the underlying spatial structure. To this end, we introduce a new perspective by formulating the optimization of 3DGS as a primitive denoising process and propose Denoising-GS, a spatial-aware denoising framework for Gaussian primitives by taking both the positions and spatial structure into consideration. Specifically, we design an optimizer that preserves the spatial optimization flow of primitives, facilitating coherent and directed denoising rather than random perturbations. Building upon this, the Spatial Gradient-based Denoising strategy jointly considers the spatial supports of primitives to ensure gradient-consistent updates. Furthermore, the Uncertainty-based Denoising module estimates primitive-wise uncertainty to prune redundant or noisy primitives, while the Spatial Coherence Refinement strategy selectively splits primitives in sparse regions to maintain structural completeness. Experiments conducted on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that Denoising-GS consistently enhances NVS fidelity while maintaining representation compactness, achieving state-of-the-art performance across all benchmarks. Source code and models will be made publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 近期3D高斯溅射（3DGS）的进展在高保真新视角合成（NVS）中取得了显著成功，然而由于运动恢复结构（SfM）点云的稀疏性和不完整性，优化过程不可避免地会引入噪声高斯原始素。大多数现有方法仅关注优化中调整原始素的位置，而忽视了其潜在的空间结构。为此，我们引入了一个新视角，将3DGS的优化形式化为原始素去噪过程，并提出了Denoising-GS，一个空间感知的高斯原始素去噪框架，同时考虑了位置和空间结构。具体而言，我们设计了一个优化器，保留原始素的空间优化流，促进连贯且定向的去噪而非随机扰动。在此基础上，空间梯度去噪策略联合考虑原始素的空间支持以确保梯度一致性更新。此外，不确定性去噪模块通过估计原始素级别的不确定性来修剪冗余或噪声原始素，而空间一致性精炼策略则选择性地在稀疏区域分裂原始素以保持结构完整性。在三个基准数据集上进行的实验表明，Denoising-GS在保持表示紧凑性的同时一致提升了NVS保真度，在所有基准上达到了最先进性能。源代码和模型将公开发布。

</details>


### [80] [Masked Next-Scale Prediction for Self-supervised Scene Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14885)
*Zhuohao Chen,Zeng Li,Yifei Zhang,Chang Liu,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MNSP框架，通过联合学习跨尺度预测和掩码图像重建，显式建模场景文本的跨尺度结构演化，在多个基准上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自监督方法（如MIM）仅在单一空间尺度上操作，无法捕捉场景文本固有的层级结构（从粗粒度布局到细粒度笔画），导致对文本结构的建模不充分。

Method: 提出Masked Next-Scale Prediction (MNSP)框架，包含：(1) Next-Scale Prediction (NSP)模块，从低分辨率上下文预测高分辨率特征以学习层级表示；(2) 联合掩码图像重建，通过局部约束引导注意力聚焦于文本区域；(3) 多尺度语言学对齐模块，维护不同分辨率间的语义一致性。

Result: 在Union14M基准上达到86.2%平均准确率，在六个标准数据集上达到96.7%，在极端尺度和布局变化下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MNSP通过显式建模跨尺度结构演化，有效解决了单一尺度方法的局限性，为场景文本识别提供了一种统一的自监督学习框架，并取得了SOTA性能。

Original Abstract: Scene Text Recognition requires modeling visual structures that evolve from coarse layouts to fine-grained character strokes. Training such models relies on large amounts of annotated data. Recent self-supervised approaches, such as Masked Image Modeling (MIM), alleviate this dependency by leveraging large-scale unlabeled data. Yet most existing MIM methods operate at a single spatial scale and fail to capture the hierarchical nature of scene text. In this work, we introduce Masked Next-Scale Prediction (MNSP), a unified self-supervised framework designed to explicitly model cross-scale structural evolution. The framework incorporates Next-Scale Prediction (NSP), which learns hierarchical representations by predicting higher-resolution features from lower-resolution contexts. Naive scale prediction, however, tends to produce spatially diffuse attention, directing the model toward background regions rather than textual structures. MNSP resolves this limitation by jointly learning cross-scale prediction and masked image reconstruction. NSP captures global layout priors across resolutions, while masked reconstruction imposes strong local constraints that guide attention toward informative text regions. A Multi-scale Linguistic Alignment module further maintains semantic consistency across different resolutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MNSP achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 86.2\% average accuracy on the challenging Union14M benchmark and 96.7\% across six standard datasets. Additional analyses show that our method improves robustness under extreme scale and layout variations. Code is available at https://github.com/CzhczhcHczh/MNSP

中文摘要翻译: 场景文本识别需要对从粗粒度布局到细粒度笔画演化的视觉结构进行建模。训练此类模型依赖于大量标注数据。最近的自监督方法（如掩码图像建模MIM）通过利用大规模无标注数据缓解了这一依赖。然而，现有的大多数MIM方法仅在单一空间尺度上操作，无法捕捉场景文本的层级特性。本文提出掩码下一尺度预测（MNSP），一个统一的自监督框架，旨在显式建模跨尺度结构演化。该框架包含下一尺度预测（NSP），通过从低分辨率上下文预测更高分辨率特征来学习层级表示。然而，朴素的尺度预测倾向于产生空间弥散的注意力，将模型引向背景区域而非文本结构。MNSP通过联合学习跨尺度预测和掩码图像重建来解决这一局限。NSP捕获跨分辨率的全局布局先验，而掩码重建施加强大的局部约束，引导注意力聚焦于信息丰富的文本区域。多尺度语言学对齐模块进一步维护不同分辨率间的语义一致性。大量实验表明，MNSP达到了最先进的性能，在具有挑战性的Union14M基准上取得86.2%的平均准确率，在六个标准数据集上达到96.7%。额外分析表明，我们的方法在极端尺度和布局变化下具有更强的鲁棒性。代码已开源：https://github.com/CzhczhcHczh/MNSP

</details>


### [81] [Your CLIP has 164 dimensions of noise: Exploring the embeddings covariance eigenspectrum of contrastively pretrained vision-language transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14893)
*Jakub Grzywaczewski,Dawid Płudowski,Przemysław Biecek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过谱分解方法发现，对比预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）的潜在空间中存在与多模态语义信号共享的噪声子空间，且该噪声具有跨数据子集的强子群不变性；移除这些噪声维度不仅无害，还能保持或提升下游任务性能，表明VLM的潜在几何结构很大程度上由架构级别的共享噪声主导。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 对比预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）虽作为强大的特征提取器，但其共享潜在空间存在结构异常，并携带非语义的多模态噪声。现有方法缺乏对这种噪声结构的系统理解，因此本文旨在解析VLM潜在空间中的语义信号与噪声成分，揭示噪声的几何特性及其对模型表示的影响。

Method: 对VLM潜在空间的协方差矩阵进行谱分解，将其分解为多模态语义信号分量和共享噪声子空间；并通过跨不同数据子集分析噪声几何结构的子群不变性，验证去除共享噪声维度对下游任务性能的影响。

Result: 噪声几何结构在不同数据子集间表现出强子群不变性；裁剪这些共享噪声维度对下游任务性能无害，甚至能保持或主动提升任务表现。

Conclusion: VLM潜在空间中相当大一部分几何结构由共享的、架构级别的噪声所主导，而非仅由任务相关的语义信号决定；通过分离语义信号与伪影噪声，该工作为现代VLM的表征结构提供了新的机制性见解。

Original Abstract: Contrastively pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) serve as powerful feature extractors. Yet, their shared latent spaces are prone to structural anomalies and act as repositories for non-semantic, multi-modal noise. To address this phenomenon, we employ spectral decomposition of covariance matrices to decompose the VLM latent space into a multi-modal semantic signal component and a shared noise subspace. We observe that this noise geometry exhibits strong subgroup invariance across distinct data subsets. Crucially, pruning these shared noise dimensions is mainly harmless, preserving or actively improving downstream task performance. By isolating true semantic signals from artifactual noise, this work provides new mechanistic insights into the representational structure of modern VLMs, suggesting that a substantial fraction of their latent geometry is governed by shared, architecture-level noise rather than task-relevant semantics alone.

中文摘要翻译: 对比预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）作为强大的特征提取器，然而，它们的共享潜在空间易出现结构异常，并充当非语义的多模态噪声的存储库。为解决这一现象，我们采用协方差矩阵的谱分解，将VLM潜在空间分解为多模态语义信号分量和共享噪声子空间。我们观察到，该噪声几何在不同数据子集间表现出强的子群不变性。关键在于，剪除这些共享噪声维度基本无害，能够保持甚至主动提升下游任务性能。通过将真实语义信号与伪影噪声分离开，本文为现代VLM的表征结构提供了新的机制性见解，表明其潜在几何中相当大一部分由共享的、架构级别的噪声主导，而非仅由任务相关的语义信号决定。

</details>


### [82] [SEDiT: Mask-Free Video Subtitle Erasure via One-step Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14894)
*Zheng Hui,Yunlong Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEDiT是一种基于一步扩散Transformer的掩码无需求、单阶段视频字幕擦除方法，通过理论证明一步去噪的可行性，并采用混合训练策略和块流式推理实现高效的长视频处理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频编辑方法依赖掩码输入，需要预先提取目标视频掩码，分割精度直接影响完成质量；且两阶段处理存在次优性问题。字幕擦除作为局部编辑任务，像素变化小，分布偏移小，适合一步生成。

Method: 1) 提出掩码无关推理，直接擦除目标字幕；2) 采用一步扩散Transformer，利用修正流进行一步采样；3) 理论证明在局部编辑结构下条件最优传输映射和修正流速度场关于潜变量Lipschitz连续，支撑一步采样可行性；4) 混合训练策略：偶尔以干净首帧潜变量为条件，促进时间连续性；5) 直接将原始视频输入SEDiT，避免裁剪重插入导致的接缝问题；6) 块流式推理支持无限长度原生1440p视频处理。

Result: 实验验证了一步去噪的可靠性，提供了正式的理论证明；该方法能够高效处理原生1440p无限长度视频。

Conclusion: SEDiT通过一步扩散Transformer和掩码无关推理，有效解决了视频字幕擦除中掩码依赖、两阶段次优性及长视频时间一致性问题，实现了高效高质量的字幕擦除。

Original Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in video diffusion models have significantly accelerated the development of video editing techniques. However, existing methods often rely on inpainting video frames based on masked input, which requires extracting the target video mask in advance, and the precision of the segmentation directly affects the quality of the completion. In this paper, we present SEDiT, a novel one-stage video Subtitle Erasure approach via One-step Diffusion Transformer. We introduce a mask-free inference approach that enables direct erasure of the targeted subtitle. The proposed one-stage framework mitigates the sub-optimality inherent in the two-stage processing of prior models. Since subtitle removal is a localized editing task in which most pixels remain unchanged, the underlying distribution shift is minimal, making it well-suited to one-step generation under rectified flow. We empirically validate the reliability of one-step denoising and further provide a formal theoretical justification. Under the localized-editing structure of subtitle removal, the conditional optimal transport (OT) map and its induced rectified flow velocity field are Lipschitz continuous with respect to the latent variable, which underpins the theoretical feasibility of one-step sampling. To address the challenge of long-term temporal consistency, we adopt a hybrid training strategy by occasionally conditioning the model with a clean first-frame latent. This facilitates temporal continuity, allowing each segment during inference to leverage the output of its predecessor. To avoid visible seams caused by cropping and reinserting processed targets, particularly in scenarios involving substantial motion, we feed the original video directly into SEDiT. Thanks to one-step and chunk-wise streaming inference, our method can efficiently handle native 1440p video with infinite length.

中文摘要翻译: 视频扩散模型的最新突破显著加速了视频编辑技术的发展。然而，现有方法通常依赖于基于掩码输入的视频帧修复，这需要预先提取目标视频掩码，且分割精度直接影响完成质量。在本文中，我们提出SEDiT，一种新颖的基于一步扩散Transformer的单阶段视频字幕擦除方法。我们引入了一种掩码无关推理方法，能够直接擦除目标字幕。所提出的单阶段框架缓解了先前模型两阶段处理固有的次优性问题。由于字幕移除是一种局部编辑任务，大部分像素保持不变，因此潜在分布偏移极小，使其非常适合在修正流下进行一步生成。我们通过实验验证了一步去噪的可靠性，并进一步提供了正式的理论证明。在字幕移除的局部编辑结构下，条件最优传输映射及其诱导的修正流速度场关于潜变量是Lipschitz连续的，这奠定了一步采样的理论基础。为解决长期时间一致性的挑战，我们采用了一种混合训练策略，偶尔以干净首帧潜变量作为模型条件。这促进了时间连续性，使得推理过程中每个片段都能利用其前驱的输出。为避免因裁剪和重新插入处理目标而产生的可见接缝问题，特别是在涉及大幅运动的情况下，我们将原始视频直接输入SEDiT。得益于一步和块流式推理，我们的方法能够高效处理原生1440p无限长度视频。

</details>


### [83] [SteerSeg: Attention Steering for Reasoning Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14908)
*Ali Cheraghian,Hamidreza Dastmalchi,Abdelwahed Khamis,Morteza Saberi,Aijun An,Lars Petersson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SteerSeg通过输入级条件调控注意力分布，利用可学习软提示和思维链提示解决LVLM注意力分散问题，实现无需微调的视频推理分割，在多个基准上取得优异泛化性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于冻结LVLM的视频推理分割方法通过提取注意力图作为空间先验，但这些注意力图面向文本生成优化而非空间定位，导致定位信号模糊、分散，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: SteerSeg包含两个核心组件：1) 可学习软提示（soft prompts）重塑注意力分布，生成更集中的空间注意力图；2) 推理引导的思维链（CoT）提示，利用推导出的属性区分相似目标。注意力图转换为点提示引导分割模型，并通过相关性评分排序选择候选轨迹。LVLM和分割模型参数冻结，仅学习少量软提示。

Result: 仅在Ref-YouTube-VOS上训练，SteerSeg在多个不同基准上表现优异，显著提升了LVLM的空间定位能力，具有良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 注意力错配是基于注意力定位的关键瓶颈，通过输入级条件调控（软提示+CoT提示）可有效解决，在保持LVLM预训练推理能力的同时大幅提升空间定位质量。

Original Abstract: Video reasoning segmentation requires localizing objects across video frames from natural language expressions, often involving spatial reasoning and implicit references. Recent approaches leverage frozen large vision-language models (LVLMs) by extracting attention maps and using them as spatial priors for segmentation, enabling training-free grounding. However, these attention maps are optimized for text generation rather than spatial localization, often resulting in diffuse and ambiguous grounding signals. In this work, we introduce SteerSeg, a lightweight framework that identifies attention misalignment as the key bottleneck in attention-based grounding and proposes to steer attention at its source through input-level conditioning. SteerSeg combines learnable soft prompts with reasoning-guided Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting. The soft prompts reshape the attention distribution to produce more spatially concentrated maps, while CoT-derived attributes resolve ambiguity among similar objects by guiding attention toward the correct instance. The resulting attention maps are converted into point prompts across keyframes to guide a segmentation model, while candidate tracklets are ranked and selected using correlation-based scoring. Our approach freezes the LVLM and segmentation model parameters and learns only a small set of soft prompts, preserving the model's pretrained reasoning capabilities while significantly improving grounding. Despite being trained only on Ref-YouTube-VOS, SteerSeg generalizes well across diverse benchmarks, significantly improving the spatial grounding capability of LVLMs. Project page: https://steerseg.github.io

中文摘要翻译: 视频推理分割需要根据自然语言表达在视频帧中定位目标对象，通常涉及空间推理和隐式指代。现有方法利用冻结的大视觉语言模型（LVLM），通过提取注意力图并将其作为分割的空间先验，从而实现无需训练的定位。然而，这些注意力图是为文本生成而非空间定位优化的，往往产生弥散且模糊的定位信号。在本工作中，我们提出SteerSeg，一个轻量级框架，它识别出注意力错配是基于注意力定位的关键瓶颈，并提出通过输入级条件调控从源头引导注意力。SteerSeg结合了可学习软提示与推理引导的思维链（CoT）提示。软提示重塑注意力分布以生成更集中的空间图，而CoT推导出的属性通过引导注意力指向正确实例来解决相似目标间的歧义。最终的注意力图在关键帧上转换为点提示以引导分割模型，同时通过基于相关性的评分对候选轨迹进行排序和选择。我们的方法冻结LVLM和分割模型参数，仅学习少量软提示，在显著改善定位能力的同时保留了模型预训练的推理能力。尽管仅在Ref-YouTube-VOS上训练，SteerSeg在多个不同基准上表现出良好的泛化能力，显著提升了LVLM的空间定位能力。项目页面：https://steerseg.github.io

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [Physics-Grounded Adversarial Stain Augmentation with Calibrated Coverage Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13889)
*Mingi Hong*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出校准对抗性染色增强（CASA），在Macenko染色参数空间中进行对抗性增强，并通过DKW不等式利用多中心统计数据校准预算，以解决组织病理学模型在不同医院间的染色变异问题。在Camelyon17-WILDS上，CASA以93.9%的准确率优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有染色增强方法在颜色空间中采用任意超参数进行扰动，缺乏原则性的预算约束和对未见中心数据覆盖的保证，导致组织病理学模型在部署时因医院间染色差异而性能下降。

Method: 提出校准对抗性染色增强（CASA），在Macenko染色参数空间中进行对抗性增强，并通过DKW不等式利用多中心统计信息对增强预算进行校准，以确保对未见中心数据的覆盖保证。

Result: 在Camelyon17-WILDS数据集上（5个随机种子），CASA达到93.9%±1.6%的切片级准确率，优于HED-strong（88.4%）、RandStainNA（85.2%）和ERM（63.9%）；且在10种对比方法中取得最高的最差组准确率（84.9%±0.9%）。

Conclusion: CASA通过原则性的校准预算和对抗性增强，有效解决了染色变异带来的域泛化问题，显著提升了组织病理学模型在未见中心的泛化性能。

Original Abstract: Stain variation across hospitals degrades histopathology models at deployment. Existing augmentation methods perturb color spaces with arbitrary hyperparameters, lacking both a principled budget and coverage guarantees for unseen centers. We propose \textbf{C}alibrated \textbf{A}dversarial \textbf{S}tain \textbf{A}ugmentation (\textbf{CASA}), which performs adversarial augmentation in the Macenko stain parameter space with a budget calibrated from multi-center statistics via the DKW inequality. On Camelyon17-WILDS (5 seeds), CASA achieves $93.9\% \pm 1.6\%$ slide-level accuracy -- outperforming HED-strong ($88.4\% \pm 7.3\%$), RandStainNA ($85.2\% \pm 6.7\%$), and ERM ($63.9\% \pm 11.3\%$) -- with the highest worst-group accuracy ($84.9\% \pm 0.9\%$) among all 10 compared methods.

中文摘要翻译: 不同医院之间的染色变异会导致组织病理学模型在部署时性能下降。现有的增强方法使用任意超参数对颜色空间进行扰动，既缺乏原则性的预算约束，也无法保证对未见数据中心的覆盖。我们提出校准对抗性染色增强（CASA），该方法在Macenko染色参数空间中进行对抗性增强，并通过DKW不等式利用多中心统计数据校准预算。在Camelyon17-WILDS数据集上（5个随机种子），CASA达到了93.9%±1.6%的切片级准确率——优于HED-strong（88.4%±7.3%）、RandStainNA（85.2%±6.7%）和ERM（63.9%±11.3%）——并且在所有10种对比方法中取得了最高的最差组准确率（84.9%±0.9%）。

</details>


### [85] [ForcingDAS: Unified and Robust Data Assimilation via Diffusion Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14285)
*Yixuan Jia,Siyi Chen,Yida Pan,Xiao Li,Lianghe Shi,Chanyong Jung,Haijie Yuan,Ismail Alkhouri,Yue Cynthia Wu,Saiprasad Ravishankar,Jeffrey A Fessler,Qing Qu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出ForcingDAS框架，通过扩散强制（Diffusion Forcing）为每帧分配独立噪声水平，学习联合轨迹先验而非帧到帧转换，统一了滤波和平滑两种数据同化范式，在多个基准上达到或超越专用方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据同化方法面临两个问题：1）滤波方法依赖帧间转换模型，在观测非马尔可夫时（如真实天气数据中观测仅为高维隐状态的部分切片）会积累长期误差；2）学习方法通常只针对滤波（临近预报、实时预测）或平滑（回溯再分析）单一模式，割裂了本应共享的先验知识。

Method: 基于扩散强制（Diffusion Forcing）框架，为每一帧分配独立的噪声水平，学习联合轨迹先验（joint-trajectory prior）而非帧到帧转换模型。推理时通过调整推断调度，无需重新训练即可覆盖从滤波到平滑的完整谱系，包括临近预报、固定滞后平滑和批量再分析。

Result: 在2D Navier-Stokes涡度、降水临近预报和全球大气状态估计三个任务上评估，单个ForcingDAS模型与专用于单一模式的经典及学习方法相比具有竞争力或更优，在真实天气基准上增益最大。

Conclusion: ForcingDAS通过联合轨迹先验和扩散强制框架，统一了数据同化中的滤波与平滑范式，有效缓解了长期误差累积问题，且无需为不同任务重新训练模型。

Original Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) estimates the state of an evolving dynamical system from noisy, partial observations, and is widely used in scientific simulation as well as weather and climate science. In practice, filtering methods rely on frame-to-frame transition models. However, these models are fragile when observations are non-Markovian (when they form only a partial slice of a higher-dimensional latent state as in real-world weather data): they tend to accumulate errors over long horizons. At the same time, learned DA methods typically commit to a single regime, either filtering (nowcasting, real-time forecasting) or smoothing (retrospective reanalysis), which splits what should be a shared prior across application-specific pipelines. To address both issues, we introduce ForcingDAS, a unified and robust DA framework. Built on Diffusion Forcing with an independent noise level assigned to each frame, ForcingDAS learns a joint-trajectory prior instead of frame-to-frame transitions. This allows it to capture long-horizon temporal dependencies and reduce error accumulation. In addition, the same trained model spans the full filtering to smoothing spectrum at inference time. Specifically, nowcasting, fixed-lag smoothing, and batch reanalysis are selected through the inference schedule alone, without retraining. We evaluate ForcingDAS on 2D Navier-Stokes vorticity, precipitation nowcasting, and global atmospheric state estimation. Across all settings, a single model is competitive with or outperforms both learned and classical baselines that are specialized for individual regimes, with the largest gains observed on real-world weather benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 数据同化（DA）通过含噪声的部分观测来估计演化动力系统的状态，广泛用于科学模拟以及天气与气候科学。在实践中，滤波方法依赖于帧到帧的转换模型。然而，当观测是非马尔可夫时（即观测仅为真实天气数据中高维隐状态的部分切片），这些模型很脆弱：它们会在长时间跨度上积累误差。同时，学习方法通常只致力于单一范式，要么是滤波（临近预报、实时预测），要么是平滑（回溯再分析），这割裂了本应在不同应用管线间共享的先验知识。为同时解决这两个问题，我们提出了ForcingDAS——一个统一且鲁棒的数据同化框架。ForcingDAS基于扩散强制（Diffusion Forcing），为每一帧分配独立的噪声水平，学习联合轨迹先验而非帧到帧转换。这使得它能够捕获长时间跨度的时序依赖并减少误差累积。此外，同一训练好的模型在推理时即可覆盖从滤波到平滑的完整谱系。具体地，临近预报、固定滞后平滑和批量再分析仅通过推断调度的选择即可实现，无需重新训练。我们在2D Navier-Stokes涡度、降水临近预报和全球大气状态估计上评估了ForcingDAS。在所有设置中，单个模型与专用于各单一范式的学习和经典基线相比具有竞争力或表现更优，其中在真实天气基准上获得的增益最大。

</details>


### [86] [Efficient Dense Matching for Enhanced Gaussian Splatting Using AV1 Motion Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14629)
*Julien Zouein,Vibhoothi Vibhoothi,François Pitié,Anil Kokaram*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于AV1视频编码器的特征检测与匹配流水线，替代传统的COLMAP-SfM初始化，生成稠密八倍的点云，使3DGS重建的VMAF提升9点，训练时间平均减少63%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 3DGS的重建质量严重依赖初始点云质量，而传统的COLMAP-SfM管线计算成本高且在无纹理区域稀疏，导致后续重建精度和收敛速度下降。因此需要一种更高效、更稠密的初始化方法。

Method: 利用AV1视频编码器固有的运动矢量进行特征检测与匹配，绕过计算密集的穷举匹配步骤，在保持几何鲁棒性的同时生成比经典SfM稠密多达八倍的点云，用于3DGS初始化。

Result: 相比传统SfM，点云数量提升至8倍；3DGS重建的VMAF评分提升9点；达到基线质量所需的训练时间平均减少63%。

Conclusion: 基于AV1运动矢量的特征匹配流水线可显著降低SfM计算开销，生成更稠密的初始点云，从而直接提升3DGS的重建质量和训练效率。

Original Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a prominent framework for real-time, photorealistic scene reconstruction, offering significant speed-ups over Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF). However, the fidelity of 3DGS representations remains heavily dependent on the quality of the initial point cloud. While standard Structure-from-Motion (SfM) pipelines using COLMAP provide adequate initialisation, they often suffer from high computational costs and sparsity in textureless regions, which degrades subsequent reconstruction accuracy and convergence speed. In this work, we introduce an AV1-based feature detection and matching pipeline that significantly reduces SfM processing overhead. By leveraging motion vectors inherent to the AV1 video codec, we bypass computationally expensive exhaustive matching while maintaining geometric robustness. Our pipeline produces substantially denser point clouds, with up to eight times as many points as classical SfM. We demonstrate that this enhanced initialisation directly improves 3DGS performance, yielding an 9-point increase in VMAF and a 63% average reduction in training time required to reach baseline quality. The project page: https://sigmedia.tv/AV1-3DGS.github.io/

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯抛雪（3DGS）已成为实时、逼真场景重建的重要框架，相比神经辐射场（NeRF）带来了显著的加速。然而，3DGS表示的重建质量仍然严重依赖于初始点云的质量。虽然使用COLMAP的标准运动恢复结构（SfM）流水线能够提供足够的初始化，但该方法通常存在计算成本高、在无纹理区域稀疏等问题，从而降低了后续的重建精度和收敛速度。在本工作中，我们引入了一种基于AV1的特征检测与匹配流水线，显著降低了SfM的处理开销。通过利用AV1视频编码器固有的运动矢量，我们绕过了计算密集的穷举匹配，同时保持了几何鲁棒性。我们的流水线能够生成显著更稠密的点云，其点数可达经典SfM的八倍。我们证明，这种增强的初始化直接提升了3DGS的性能，使VMAF评分提升了9点，并使得达到基线质量所需的训练时间平均减少63%。项目页面：https://sigmedia.tv/AV1-3DGS.github.io/

</details>


### [87] [Denoising for Neuromorphic Cameras Based on Graph Spectral Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14734)
*Shimpei Harada,Junya Hara,Hiroshi Higashi,Yuichi Tanaka*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图谱特征的神经形态相机（事件相机）去噪方法，通过构建事件图、利用事件密度先验计算图参数、并自定义图拉普拉斯算子以加速特征分解，从而有效去除噪声事件。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 事件相机虽具有高时间分辨率、低延迟、低功耗和高动态范围等优点，但其高灵敏度导致测量结果中包含大量噪声，影响后续应用性能，因此需要有效的去噪方法。

Method: 首先构建事件图，节点为事件，边表示事件间的时空距离；利用三维事件密度先验计算控制图连通性的图参数；然后计算图拉普拉斯矩阵的特征向量，利用这些特征向量直接提取无噪声事件；在计算特征向量时，自定义图拉普拉斯算子以重排特征值，从而可采用快速特征求解器替代朴素特征分解，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界事件数据的实验表明，所提方法相比其他方法能有效去除原始事件中的噪声事件。

Conclusion: 基于图谱特征的去噪方法能有效去除事件相机数据中的噪声，且通过自定义图拉普拉斯算子提高了计算效率。

Original Abstract: Neuromorphic cameras, also known as event-based cameras, can detect changes in the environmental brightness asynchronously and independently for each pixel. They output the brightness changes, i.e., events, as 3-D (2-D pixel coordinates + time) streaming data. While event-based cameras are used in many applications because of their desirable characteristics, e.g., high temporal resolution, low latency, low power consumption, and high dynamic range, their measurements contain considerable noise due to their high sensitivity. In this paper, we propose a denoising method for event-based cameras based on graph spectral features. In the proposed method, we first construct a graph where nodes represent events and edges represent the spatiotemporal distance between the events. To calculate the graph-specified parameter that controls the connectivities of a constructed graph, we utilize the prior on the density of 3-D events. We then calculate the eigenvectors of the graph Laplacian. The obtained eigenvectors are used to extract noiseless events directly. In the calculation of the eigenvectors, we customize the graph Laplacian to reorder its eigenvalues. This allows us to leverage fast eigensolver algorithms instead of the naive eigendecomposition and thereby reduce computational complexity. In experiments on synthetic and real-world event data, we demonstrate that the proposed method effectively removes noise events from the raw events compared to alternative methods.

中文摘要翻译: 神经形态相机，也称为事件相机，能够异步、独立地检测每个像素的环境亮度变化。它们输出亮度变化（即事件）作为三维（二维像素坐标+时间）流数据。虽然事件相机因其理想特性（如高时间分辨率、低延迟、低功耗和高动态范围）被应用于许多场景，但其高灵敏度导致测量结果中包含大量噪声。本文提出了一种基于图谱特征的事件相机去噪方法。在所提方法中，我们首先构建一个图，其中节点表示事件，边表示事件之间的时空距离。为了计算控制所构建图连通性的图特定参数，我们利用了三维事件密度的先验信息。然后计算图拉普拉斯矩阵的特征向量，获得的特征向量用于直接提取无噪声事件。在特征向量计算过程中，我们自定义图拉普拉斯算子以重排其特征值。这使得我们能够利用快速特征求解器替代朴素特征分解，从而降低计算复杂度。在合成数据和真实世界事件数据上的实验表明，与替代方法相比，所提方法能有效去除原始事件中的噪声事件。

</details>


### [88] [FaSST: Fast Sparsifying Secondary Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15086)
*Darukeesan Pakiyarajah,Samuel Fernández-Menduiña,Eduardo Pavez,Antonio Ortega,Debargha Mukherjee*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低复杂度的数据依赖二次变换框架FaSST，通过Givens旋转分解稀疏正交变换，在AV2帧内预测残差编码中，相比LFNST减少83.67%计算量，同时避免固定系数截断，最高节省1.80% BD-rate。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据依赖二次变换（如LFNST）存在两个主要问题：(1) 丢弃高频二次变换系数，限制了编码增益；(2) 缺乏显式的率失真优化设计准则。此外，计算复杂度也限制了其实际部署。

Method: 提出Fast Sparsifying Secondary Transforms (FaSSTs)框架：将数据驱动的稀疏正交变换（SOT）分解为一系列Givens旋转的序列；采用交替最小化策略结合近似Givens分解过程高效求解旋转角度；根据预测模式自适应调整旋转数量以降低计算复杂度；为AV2帧内预测残差设计模式依赖的二次变换。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 模式自适应FaSST在匹配LFNST率失真性能的同时，计算量减少83.67%；(2) 通过避免固定系数截断，FaSST相比LFNST最高节省1.80% BD-rate，同时计算复杂度降低66.24%。

Conclusion: FaSST作为一种低复杂度、率失真优化的数据依赖二次变换框架，能够在显著降低计算复杂度的同时保持或超越现有方法的编码性能，为视频编码中的变换设计提供了新的有效方案。

Original Abstract: Data-dependent secondary transforms, which aim to decorrelate coefficients of a separable primary transform, can improve residual coding efficiency; however, their deployment is often constrained by computational complexity. Recent video codecs use variants of the low-frequency non-separable transform (LFNST), which discards some high-frequency secondary transform coefficients, limiting achievable coding gains. Moreover, existing data-dependent secondary transforms lack explicit rate-distortion (RD) optimal design criteria. In this work, we propose a framework for designing low-complexity data-dependent secondary transforms, termed Fast Sparsifying Secondary Transforms (FaSSTs). Our approach approximates data-driven sparse orthonormal transforms (SOTs) by factorizing them into a sequence of Givens rotations. The rotations are efficiently determined using an alternating minimization strategy combined with an approximate Givens factorization procedure. Our method adapts the number of rotations based on the prediction mode, further reducing computational complexity. We design mode-dependent secondary transforms for intra-prediction residuals in AV2 using FaSST. Experimental results show that mode-adaptive FaSST matches the RD performance of LFNST while reducing the number of computations by 83.67%. Moreover, by avoiding fixed-coefficient truncation, FaSST achieves up to 1.80% BD-rate savings relative to LFNST while operating at 66.24% lower complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 数据依赖的二次变换旨在对可分离一次变换的系数进行去相关，能够提升残差编码效率；然而，其部署常常受到计算复杂度的限制。近年来的视频编码器使用低频非分离变换（LFNST）的变体，该变换丢弃部分高频二次变换系数，限制了可实现的编码增益。此外，现有的数据依赖二次变换缺乏显式的率失真（RD）最优设计准则。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种用于设计低复杂度数据依赖二次变换的框架，称为快速稀疏化二次变换（FaSSTs）。我们的方法通过将数据驱动的稀疏正交变换（SOT）分解为一系列Givens旋转序列来逼近该变换。这些旋转通过交替最小化策略结合近似Givens分解过程高效确定。我们的方法根据预测模式自适应调整旋转数量，进一步降低计算复杂度。我们使用FaSST为AV2中的帧内预测残差设计了模式依赖的二次变换。实验结果表明，模式自适应的FaSST在匹配LFNST的率失真性能的同时，将计算量减少了83.67%。此外，通过避免固定系数截断，FaSST相比LFNST最高实现了1.80%的BD-rate节省，同时计算复杂度降低了66.24%。

</details>
