### [89] [FMCW Lidar Beyond Nyquist by Instantaneous Frequency Fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14039)
*Alfred Krister Ulvog,Joshua Rapp,Vivek K Goyal*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对FMCW激光雷达在距离和速度估计中受限于频率混叠和相位噪声的问题，提出匹配滤波和瞬时频率拟合两种信号处理方法，能够在频域混叠条件下恢复更大范围的距离与速度，且对任意频率调制具有灵活性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统FMCW激光雷达依赖恒差拍频率和FFT峰值检测，受奈奎斯特采样定理限制，最大可测距离有限；且线性调频光源或重采样方案增加了硬件复杂度。此外，相位噪声会严重干扰信号处理，现有方法鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出两种信号处理方法：一是匹配滤波（matched filtering），通过全波形处理恢复距离和速度；二是瞬时频率拟合（instantaneous frequency fitting），通过考虑相位偏差增强对相位噪声的鲁棒性。两种方法均适用于任意频率调制，无需线性扫频光源。

Result: 通过仿真实验和误指定Cramér–Rao界（misspecified CRB）理论分析表明，瞬时频率拟合法相比匹配滤波对相位噪声更鲁棒；两种方法均能在频域混叠条件下恢复更大范围的距离和速度；非线性调制相比传统三角调制可提供更优的距离和速度灵敏度。

Conclusion: 所提出的匹配滤波和瞬时频率拟合法突破了传统FMCW激光雷达的采样混叠限制，能够在不依赖线性调频光源的情况下恢复更大范围的距离与速度，且瞬时频率拟合法对相位噪声具有更强的鲁棒性。该方法支持任意非线性调制，为提升FMCW激光雷达性能提供了新的信号处理路径。

Original Abstract: Frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) lidar conventionally estimates distance and velocity from constant beat frequencies generated through interferometry. Existing FMCW implementations emphasize simple signal processing -- e.g., beat frequency estimation via a fast Fourier transform (FFT) algorithm plus peak-finding -- which results in hardware-focused solutions requiring linear swept-frequency laser sources or linearized resampling. However, the maximum achievable distance by this method is limited by the need to sample the interference signal without aliasing. In this work, we propose two signal processing methods: matched filtering and instantaneous frequency fitting. These two methods can recover larger ranges of distance and velocity by considering the full waveform despite aliasing in the frequency domain. Furthermore, the FMCW lidar signal is often corrupted by phase noise, and we show that the instantaneous frequency fitting approach is more robust than matched filtering by considering the deviation in the phase. We present comprehensive simulation studies along with theoretical analysis using the misspecified Cramér--Rao bound. As these methods are flexible to arbitrary frequency modulation, we also show results for non-linear modulations that could yield better sensitivity to distance and velocity compared to the popular triangular modulation.

中文摘要翻译: 调频连续波（FMCW）激光雷达通常通过干涉产生的恒定差拍频率来估计距离和速度。现有的FMCW实现方案强调简单的信号处理——例如通过快速傅里叶变换（FFT）算法加峰值检测进行差拍频率估计——这导致其采用硬件导向的解决方案，需要线性扫频激光源或线性化重采样。然而，该方法的可达最大距离受限于无混叠采样干涉信号的需求。在这项工作中，我们提出了两种信号处理方法：匹配滤波和瞬时频率拟合。这两种方法通过考虑全波形，即使在频域存在混叠的情况下，也能恢复更大范围的距离和速度。此外，FMCW激光雷达信号常受相位噪声污染，我们通过考虑相位偏差表明，瞬时频率拟合法比匹配滤波更具鲁棒性。我们基于误指定Cramér–Rao界进行了全面的仿真研究和理论分析。由于这些方法适用于任意频率调制，我们还展示了非线性调制的结果，相较于流行的三角调制，非线性调制能在距离和速度灵敏度方面获得更优的性能。

</details>


### [90] [UAV Energy Consumption Models for Wireless Systems Research: Model Selection and Misconceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14116)
*Mohammadreza Barzegaran,Hamid Jafarkhani*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了无人机能量消耗模型，特别关注其在无线系统研究中的应用，排除了数据驱动和过于复杂的模型，提供了实用指导，并指出了现有文献中的常见错误与空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无人机在无线系统中应用广泛，但机载能量有限严重制约其部署。准确的能量消耗建模是无人机部署和轨迹优化的关键，然而现有模型使用中存在诸多误区，亟需系统梳理和指导。

Method: 通过分类常见无人机类型和典型飞行阶段，系统综述现有能量消耗模型，分析其适用范围，指出使用中的常见错误和文献空白。

Result: 展示了使用错误模型会导致能量消耗计算出现显著误差；识别了现有模型在特定场景下的缺失。

Conclusion: 强调了为缺失场景开发能量消耗模型的必要性，为无线系统研究中正确选择和使用能量模型提供了清晰实用的指南。

Original Abstract: Uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) are gaining increasing attention in wireless systems, providing new opportunities to expand the reach and improve the quality of wireless services. Despite their versatility, UAVs are limited by available energy onboard, which results in significant challenges in deploying UAV-enabled wireless systems. Modeling energy consumption is an essential component of the deployment and trajectory optimization of UAVs. This article presents a comprehensive overview of UAV energy consumption models, with a focus on their relevance to wireless systems research. We deliberately exclude data-driven and overly complex models to provide clear and practical guidelines for their use in wireless systems research. We begin by categorizing the most common types of UAVs and describing the typical flight phases considered in the literature. We then review existing energy consumption models, focusing on their scope with respect to UAV types and flight phases. We also discuss common mistakes in the use of these models and highlight the existing gaps in the literature. In particular, we show how the use of a wrong model can lead to significant errors in energy consumption calculations. Finally, we emphasize the need to develop energy consumption models for missing scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 无人机在无线系统中日益受到关注，为扩展无线服务的覆盖范围和提高服务质量提供了新机遇。尽管无人机具有多功能性，但其机载能量有限，这给部署无人机赋能的无线系统带来了重大挑战。能量消耗建模是无人机部署和轨迹优化的重要组成部分。本文全面综述了无人机能量消耗模型，重点关注其在无线系统研究中的相关性。我们有意排除了数据驱动和过于复杂的模型，以便为其在无线系统研究中的使用提供清晰实用的指南。我们首先对最常见的无人机类型进行分类，并描述了文献中考虑的典型飞行阶段。然后，我们回顾了现有的能量消耗模型，重点关注其在无人机类型和飞行阶段方面的适用范围。我们还讨论了这些模型使用中的常见错误，并指出了现有文献中的空白。特别地，我们展示了使用错误模型会导致能量消耗计算出现显著误差。最后，我们强调了为缺失场景开发能量消耗模型的必要性。

</details>


### [91] [Multimodal Learning for MIMO Beam Prediction Based on Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14650)
*Zijian Zheng,Wenqiang Yi,Hyundong Shin,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于变分推断的多模态框架，通过两阶段训练策略（先利用单模态数据学习表示，再在有限多模态样本上进行语义对齐），仅需20%的多模态训练数据即可在DeepSense6G数据集上实现与端到端基准相当的波束预测精度和可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在通感一体化大规模MIMO系统中，多模态学习虽能提升波束预测精度，但获取语义对齐的多模态数据集成本高昂，限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出基于变分推断的多模态框架，将优化问题解耦为模块化特征提取和跨模态语义对齐；设计两阶段训练策略，先利用丰富单模态数据进行表示学习，再在有限多模态样本上进行精细化对齐。

Result: 在DeepSense6G数据集上，所提框架仅需传统端到端基准方法20%的多模态训练数据，即可达到具有竞争力的波束预测精度和可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提出的解耦式多模态学习框架能有效提升数据效率，在感知不确定性下实现鲁棒特征融合，显著降低对语义对齐多模态数据的需求。

Original Abstract: Accurate beam prediction is essential for mitigating signalling overhead and latency in integrated sensing and communication-enabled massive multi-input multi-output systems. With the aid of multimodal learning, the prediction accuracy can be enhanced by leveraging the complementary information from other existing sensors, but the practical deployment is often constrained by the high cost of acquiring semantically aligned multimodal datasets. This paper proposes a variational-inference-based multimodal framework that decouples the optimization problem into modular feature extraction and cross-modal semantic alignment. Specifically, we develop a two-stage training strategy where the model utilises abundant unimodal data for representation learning before performing refined alignment on limited multimodal samples. This design enhances data efficiency and ensures robust feature fusion under sensing uncertainties. Experimental results on the DeepSense6G dataset demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves competitive beam prediction accuracy and maintains high reliability, while only requiring 20% of the multimodal training data compared to conventional end-to-end benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 精确的波束预测对于降低通感一体化大规模多输入多输出系统中的信令开销和延迟至关重要。借助多模态学习，可以利用来自其他现有传感器的互补信息来提升预测精度，但其实际部署往往受限于获取语义对齐的多模态数据集的高昂成本。本文提出一种基于变分推断的多模态框架，将优化问题解耦为模块化特征提取和跨模态语义对齐。具体而言，我们开发了一种两阶段训练策略：模型先利用丰富的单模态数据进行表示学习，再在有限的多模态样本上进行精细化对齐。该设计提高了数据效率，并确保在感知不确定性下的鲁棒特征融合。在DeepSense6G数据集上的实验结果表明，所提框架在仅需传统端到端基准方法20%的多模态训练数据的情况下，仍能实现具有竞争力的波束预测精度并保持高可靠性。

</details>


### [92] [An integration-free approach for particle flow filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14852)
*Domonkos Csuzdi,Tamás Bécsi,Olivér Törő*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对Log-homotopy粒子流滤波器中数值积分计算成本高且易受刚性影响的问题，提出了在向量线性高斯测量下精确Daum-Huang确定性粒子流的闭合形式解析解，该解通过将ODE变换到特定特征空间推导得出，并与精确卡尔曼测量更新数学等价，同时可嵌入N步切片方法以缓解非线性测量模型中的刚性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Log-homotopy粒子流滤波器在实现非线性贝叶斯估计时，需要通过数值积分求解伪时间常微分方程，但数值积分存在计算成本高和容易受到刚性（stiffness）影响的实际问题，亟需一种无积分、闭合形式的解析解来克服这些挑战。

Method: 将控制粒子从先验向后验迁移的伪时间ODE变换到特定的特征空间中，推导出齐次状态转移矩阵和非齐次强迫项的闭合形式代数表达式，从而得到精确Daum-Huang确定性粒子流的解析解；并将该闭合形式评估嵌入N步切片方法中，用于高度非线性测量模型。

Result: 理论证明该解析解与精确卡尔曼测量更新在数学上等价；通过N步切片方法的嵌入，提供了一种缓解刚性、无需积分运算的粒子更新方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的闭合形式解析解有效规避了数值积分的计算负担和刚性问题，为线性高斯测量条件下的粒子流滤波提供了高效的精确更新方法，并可通过切片策略扩展到非线性场景。

Original Abstract: Log-homotopy particle flow filters realize nonlinear Bayesian estimation by continuously migrating samples from the prior to the posterior distribution. This transport is governed by a pseudo-time ordinary differential equation (ODE). A major practical challenge of these filters is the need for numerical integration, which suffers from high computational cost and susceptibility to stiffness. This paper develops an exact, integration-free closed-form solution for the exact Daum--Huang (EDH) deterministic particle flow under vector linear Gaussian measurements. By transforming the ODE into a specific eigenspace, closed-form algebraic expressions are derived for both the homogeneous state transition matrix and the inhomogeneous forcing term. We prove that this analytic solution is mathematically equivalent to the exact Kalman measurement update. Furthermore, we demonstrate how this closed-form evaluation can be embedded within an $N$-step slicing method, providing a stiffness-mitigating, integration-free particle update for highly nonlinear measurement models.

中文摘要翻译: Log-homotopy粒子流滤波器通过连续地将样本从先验分布迁移到后验分布来实现非线性贝叶斯估计。这种迁移由一个伪时间常微分方程（ODE）控制。这类滤波器的一个主要实际挑战是需要数值积分，而数值积分存在计算成本高且容易受到刚性影响的问题。本文针对向量线性高斯测量下的精确Daum-Huang（EDH）确定性粒子流，开发了一种精确的、无需积分的闭合形式解。通过将ODE变换到特定的特征空间，推导出了齐次状态转移矩阵和非齐次强迫项的闭合形式代数表达式。我们证明了该解析解在数学上与精确卡尔曼测量更新等价。此外，我们展示了如何将这种闭合形式评估嵌入到N步切片方法中，从而为高度非线性的测量模型提供一种缓解刚性、无需积分运算的粒子更新方法。

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在部分可观测条件下，基于视觉观测对过去时间信号时序逻辑(ptSTL)进行认证运行时监控。提出了可复用的监控接口，通过语义基向量和滚动预测两种方案，实现无需针对每个公式重新训练的监控，并提供有限样本保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有监控方法在处理视觉观测下的时序逻辑属性时，通常需要为每个公式单独训练和校准，缺乏可重用性。本文旨在设计一种可复用的监控框架，一旦训练和校准完成，就能对目标片段中的任意公式进行认证，无需针对每个公式重新训练。

Method: 提出了两种监控器：(1) 语义基监控器，利用有限时序原子字典的鲁棒性分数作为语义基向量，证明该向量是单调1-Lipschitz可复用接口类中的最小预测目标；(2) 滚动预测监控器，仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构时序历史。通过单个共形校准过程认证整个公式片段，无需联合界。

Result: 在行人十字路口基准上，滚动监控器在短时域上获得更紧的认证界，而语义基监控器在长时域上紧致度提升高达4倍。在真实Waymo驾驶数据上，两种监控器均经验证满足共形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的可复用监控框架有效解决了部分可观测条件下视觉时序逻辑监控的可重用性问题，两种监控器在不同时域长度上各具优势，且在实际自动驾驶数据上验证了其有效性。

Original Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了在部分可观测条件下，基于视觉观测对过去时间信号时序逻辑(ptSTL)进行认证运行时监控。监控器必须从图像中推断安全相关的量并提供有限样本保证，同时具备可复用性：一旦训练和校准完成，应能对目标片段中的任意公式进行认证，而无需针对每个公式重新训练。对于由有限时序原子字典导出的片段，我们证明了语义基(即原子鲁棒性分数向量)是单调1-Lipschitz可复用接口类中的最小预测目标：任意公式均由解析树导出的确定性解码器评估，且单次共形校准即可认证整个片段，无需联合界。我们还引入了一种滚动预测监控器，该监控器仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构时序历史；该方法更易学习，但在长时域上会变得保守。在行人十字路口基准上，滚动监控器在短时域上获得更紧的认证界，而语义基监控器在长时域上紧致度提升高达4倍。我们在真实Waymo驾驶数据上验证了所提出的监控器，两种监控器均经验证满足共形覆盖保证。

</details>


### [94] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAE）从三种不同架构的EEG基础模型（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）中提取稀疏特征字典，并通过临床分类法锚定特征来评估单语义性和纠缠性。引入概念引导和目标vs脱靶探测区域指标，揭示了三种操作模式，暴露了“破球”干预和临床纠缠（如年龄-病理混淆）等关键表征失败，最后通过频谱解码器将潜在操作映射为生理可解释的频谱特征。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: EEG基础模型在临床任务上表现优异，但其内部计算机制不透明，阻碍了临床信任。现有可解释性方法难以揭示模型内部表征与临床概念之间的对应关系，亟需一个系统性的框架来分析和理解EEG模型的内部表征。

Method: 在三种架构不同的EEG Transformer（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）上应用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAE），从嵌入层提取稀疏特征字典；基于临床分类法（异常、年龄、性别、用药）锚定特征，评估单语义性和纠缠性；引入单一超参数流程（由内在字典健康审计驱动）跨架构迁移；通过概念引导提出“目标vs脱靶”探针区域指标量化引导选择性；利用频谱解码器将潜在操作映射回幅度频谱。

Result: 单一超参数流程可稳健跨三种架构迁移；识别出三种操作模式：可选择引导、已编码但纠缠、未编码；发现了“破球”干预（导致全局模型性能崩溃）和临床纠缠（如年龄-病理混淆，无法在不破坏一个概念的情况下抑制另一个概念）；频谱解码器成功将潜在操作映射为生理可解释的频谱特征，如病理性慢波抑制和α波段恢复。

Conclusion: 该框架系统性地揭示了EEG基础模型内部表征的优缺点，提供了量化评估模型表征质量的方法，并通过频谱映射桥接了潜在操作与生理可解释性之间的鸿沟，为提升临床信任和模型透明性奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

中文摘要翻译: EEG基础模型在临床任务上取得了最先进的性能，但驱动其预测的内部计算机制依然不透明——这成为临床信任的障碍。我们在三种架构不同的EEG Transformer（SleepFM、REVE和LaBraM）上应用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAE），从其嵌入中提取稀疏特征字典。通过将这些特征锚定在临床分类法（异常、年龄、性别和用药）上，我们跨架构基准测试了单语义性和纠缠性。一个由内在字典健康审计驱动的单一超参数流程，能够稳健地迁移至所有三种架构。通过概念引导，我们引入了一种"目标vs脱靶"探针区域指标来量化引导选择性，并揭示了三种操作模式：可选择引导、已编码但纠缠、以及未编码。该框架暴露了关键的表征失败："破球"干预会破坏全局模型性能，以及临床纠缠（例如年龄-病理混淆），即无法在不破坏另一个概念的前提下抑制某个概念。最后，频谱解码器将这些干预映射回幅度频谱，将潜在操作转化为生理可解释的频率特征，例如病理性慢波抑制和α波段恢复。

</details>


### [95] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合变分自编码器（Joint-VAE），通过架构退火（architectural annealing）在解码前调整编码器输出，无需手动调参即可让模型自适应地平衡离散和连续潜变量，从而在dMRI结构连接组分析中有效分离采集相关变异与生物变异。在7416个连接组数据上，该方法在站点学习上显著优于基线（ARI=0.53, p<0.05）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: dMRI数据中不同站点、扫描仪和协议带来的采集差异会引入变异，干扰结构连接组分析。现有方法要么将所有变异建模为连续型（导致采集效应被吸收进连续潜空间），要么使用混合潜空间模型但需要手动容量调参。因此需要一种无需手动调参、能自动分离采集效应与生物变异的无监督框架。

Method: 提出Joint-VAE框架，核心创新是“架构退火”（architectural annealing）：在解码之前对编码器输出进行退火处理，使模型在训练过程中自适应地平衡离散和连续潜变量，无需手动调节离散/连续组件容量。对比基线包括标准VAE、PCA+k-means聚类、以及仅通过损失函数退火的混合模型。

Result: 在包含7416个结构连接组（年龄2-102岁，来自13项研究、25种独特采集参数组合）的数据集上，架构退火的Joint-VAE在站点学习任务上取得ARI=0.53（p<0.05），显著优于各基线方法。模型恢复的聚类与扫描仪和协议差异高度对齐。

Conclusion: 混合连续-离散潜空间结合架构退火（而非损失退火）是一种有效的无监督机制，能够捕获dMRI中的采集变异。Joint-VAE通过联合建模平滑结构和类别结构，可以恢复与扫描仪和协议差异对齐的聚类，为分离采集效应与生物变异提供了有力工具。

Original Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

中文摘要翻译: 不同站点、扫描仪和协议在扩散磁共振成像（dMRI）中的采集差异会引入变异性，使结构连接组分析复杂化。这激发了深度学习模型的需求，使其能够在低维空间中表示高维连接组，同时明确地将采集相关效应与生物变异分离开来。传统的降维方法将所有变异建模为连续的，因此采集效应常常被吸收进连续潜空间中。最近的混合潜空间模型通过结合离散和连续组件来解决这一问题，但通常需要手动容量调参来确保离散组件捕获预期的变异性。我们引入了一种无监督框架，通过在解码前对编码器输出进行架构退火（architectural annealing）来消除这种手动调参，使模型在训练过程中能够自适应地平衡离散和连续潜变量。为评估该方法，我们整理了一个包含7,416个源自dMRI的结构连接组数据集，年龄跨度从2岁到102岁，涵盖13项研究、25种独特的采集参数组合。其中5,900例为认知未受损者，877例为轻度认知障碍（MCI），639例为阿尔茨海默病（AD）。我们将该方法与标准VAE、PCA结合k-means聚类以及仅通过损失函数退火的混合模型进行了比较。我们的架构退火在站点学习任务上取得了优于这些基线的结果（ARI=0.53, p<0.05）。结果表明，混合连续-离散潜空间结合架构退火（而非基于损失的退火）为捕获dMRI中的采集变异性提供了一种有效的无监督机制：通过联合建模平滑结构和类别结构，Joint-VAE恢复了与扫描仪和协议差异对齐的聚类。

</details>


### [96] [Beyond Mode-Seeking RL: Trajectory-Balance Post-Training for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13935)
*Saba Ahmadi,Prasanna Parthasarathi,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TraFL，一种基于轨迹流平衡的目标函数，用于解决扩散语言模型在奖励驱动后训练中出现的“轨迹锁定”问题——即模型将概率质量过度集中到少数去噪路径上，降低了对其他正确解的覆盖。实验表明，TraFL在数学推理和代码生成基准上全面优于基线模型，且优势随采样预算增加而持续。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型的后训练方法大多采用最大化奖励的目标，这导致一个关键失败模式——“轨迹锁定”：奖励驱动的更新将概率质量过度集中到少数去噪路径上，减少了重复采样时覆盖其他正确解的可能性。

Method: 提出TraFL（Trajectory Flow baLancing），一种轨迹平衡目标函数。该目标将策略训练导向一个以冻结参考模型为锚点的奖励倾斜目标分布。具体实现包括：一个适用于扩散语言模型的扩散兼容序列级替代项，以及一个学习得到的提示相关归一化项。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，TraFL是唯一在所有基准长度设置中都能提升基线模型性能的后训练方法，且增益随采样预算增加而持续。改进可迁移至未见评估：在Minerva Math上保持优于基线模型，并在LiveCodeBench的每个难度划分中均为最强方法。

Conclusion: TraFL有效解决了扩散语言模型后训练中的轨迹锁定问题，通过轨迹流平衡目标实现了比现有方法更稳健且可扩展的性能提升，验证了该方法的通用性和有效性。

Original Abstract: Diffusion language models are a promising alternative to autoregressive models, yet post-training methods for them largely adapt reward-maximizing objectives. We identify a central failure mode in this setting we call trajectory locking: sampled reward-driven updates over-concentrate probability mass onto a narrow set of denoising paths, reducing coverage of alternative correct solutions under repeated sampling. To address this, we propose TraFL (Trajectory Flow baLancing), a trajectory-balance objective that trains the policy toward a reward-tilted target distribution anchored to a frozen reference model. We make this practical for diffusion language models with a diffusion-compatible sequence-level surrogate and a learned prompt-dependent normalization. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, TraFL is the only evaluated post-training method that improves over the base model in every benchmark-length setting, with gains that persist as the sampling budget increases. The improvements transfer to held-out evaluations: TraFL stays above the base model on Minerva Math and is the strongest method on every LiveCodeBench difficulty split.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散语言模型是自回归模型的一种有前景的替代方案，然而针对它们的后训练方法大多采用最大化奖励的目标。我们识别出该设置中的一个核心失败模式，称为“轨迹锁定”：采样的奖励驱动更新将概率质量过度集中到少数去噪路径上，从而在重复采样时减少了对其他正确解的覆盖。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了TraFL（轨迹流平衡），一种轨迹平衡目标函数，它将策略训练导向一个以冻结参考模型为锚点的奖励倾斜目标分布。我们通过一个扩散兼容的序列级替代项和一个学习得到的提示相关归一化项，使其对扩散语言模型实用化。在数学推理和代码生成基准上，TraFL是唯一在所有基准长度设置中都能提升基线模型性能的后训练方法，且增益随采样预算增加而持续。这些改进可迁移至未见评估：TraFL在Minerva Math上保持优于基线模型，并且在LiveCodeBench的每个难度划分中均为最强方法。

</details>


### [97] [EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941)
*Jiaqi Liu,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveMem提出了一种自演化记忆架构，不仅让存储内容进化，还让检索机制（评分函数、融合策略、答案生成策略）在部署后也能通过大模型驱动的诊断模块自动调整，无需手动调参。在LoCoMo和MemBench上分别超过最强基线25.7%和18.9%，且发现的配置可跨基准迁移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统将检索基础设施视为固定不变：存储内容可以演化，但评分函数、融合策略和答案生成策略在部署后保持冻结。要实现真正的自适应记忆，存储知识和检索机制需要共同演化。

Method: EvolveMem将完整的检索配置暴露为结构化动作空间，由大模型驱动的诊断模块进行优化。每一轮演化中，该模块读取每个问题的失败日志，识别根因并提出配置调整建议；受保护的元分析器应用这些调整，并配备自动回归回滚和停滞探索机制，形成闭环自演化。

Result: 在LoCoMo上，EvolveMem比最强基线提升25.7%，比最简基线提升78.0%；在MemBench上比最强基线提升18.9%。演化出的配置可在不同基准间正迁移，表明学到的是通用检索原则而非特定于基准的启发式方法。

Conclusion: EvolveMem证明了检索机制的自演化可以替代人工配置调优，从极简基线开始自主收敛到有效策略，甚至发现原始动作空间中不存在的新配置维度，且演化结果具有跨基准泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that operate across multiple sessions, yet existing memory systems treat retrieval infrastructure as fixed: stored content evolves while scoring functions, fusion strategies, and answer-generation policies remain frozen at deployment. We argue that truly adaptive memory requires co-evolution at two levels: the stored knowledge and the retrieval mechanism that queries it. We present EvolveMem, a self-evolving memory architecture that exposes its full retrieval configuration as a structured action space optimized by an LLM-powered diagnosis module. In each evolution round, the module reads per-question failure logs, identifies root causes, and proposes targeted configuration adjustments; a guarded meta-analyzer applies them with automatic revert-on-regression and explore-on-stagnation safeguards. This closed-loop self-evolution realizes an AutoResearch process: the system autonomously conducts iterative research cycles on its own architecture, replacing manual configuration tuning. Starting from a minimal baseline, the process converges autonomously, discovering effective retrieval strategies including entirely new configuration dimensions not present in the original action space. On LoCoMo, EvolveMem outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.7% relative and achieves a 78.0% relative improvement over the minimal baseline. On MemBench, EvolveMem exceeds the strongest baseline by 18.9% relative. Evolved configurations transfer across benchmarks with positive rather than catastrophic transfer, indicating that the self-evolution process captures universal retrieval principles rather than benchmark-specific heuristics. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem.

中文摘要翻译: 长期记忆对于跨多会话运行的LLM智能体至关重要，然而现有的记忆系统将检索基础设施视为固定不变：存储内容可以演化，但评分函数、融合策略和答案生成策略在部署后保持冻结。我们认为，真正的自适应记忆需要在两个层面共同演化：存储的知识以及查询它的检索机制。我们提出了EvolveMem，一种自演化记忆架构，它将完整的检索配置暴露为一个结构化的动作空间，并由大模型驱动的诊断模块进行优化。在每一轮演化中，该模块读取每个问题的失败日志，识别根本原因，并提出有针对性的配置调整建议；受保护的元分析器应用这些调整，并配备自动回归回滚和停滞探索机制。这种闭环自演化实现了一个自动研究过程：系统自主地对其自身架构进行迭代研究循环，取代了手动配置调优。从一个极简基线开始，该过程自主收敛，发现有效的检索策略，包括原始动作空间中不存在的新配置维度。在LoCoMo上，EvolveMem比最强基线相对提升25.7%，比最简基线相对提升78.0%。在MemBench上，EvolveMem比最强基线相对提升18.9%。演化得到的配置可跨基准正迁移而非灾难性迁移，表明自演化过程捕捉到的是通用检索原则，而非特定于基准的启发式方法。代码已开源：https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem。

</details>


### [98] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将加权InfoNCE目标函数解释为距离几何问题，揭示了对比学习中加权方案与嵌入几何之间的精确关系，分析了不同目标（SupCon、Soft SupCon、y-Aware CL、X-CLR）在平衡/非平衡分类和连续标签场景下的最优几何结构，为设计对比学习目标提供了理论框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 对比学习旨在通过反映相似度图来保持样本间的结构关系，但所得嵌入的几何结构仍不清晰，缺乏对加权方案如何影响嵌入几何的理论理解。

Method: 将加权InfoNCE目标函数重新解释为距离几何问题，其中加权方案指定了表示需要实现的目标几何结构，从而从理论上刻画了多种监督和弱监督对比学习目标的最优嵌入。

Result: (1) SupCon和Soft SupCon在分类中会将同类样本坍缩到单一原型，但平衡SupCon恢复经典正则单纯形几何，而类别不平衡会破坏对称性；(2) Soft SupCon在类别不平衡下仍保持正则单纯形几何；(3) y-Aware CL在连续标签设置中除非标签位于超球面上，否则无法达到其熵最优；(4) 几何一致的加权方案（如欧氏-欧氏加权或X-CLR）能产生唯一的最优嵌入。

Conclusion: 加权方案的选择决定了对比学习在几何上是否可实现、退化或不一致，为设计对比学习目标提供了理论指导框架。

Original Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

中文摘要翻译: 对比学习旨在通过学习反映相似度图的表示来保持样本之间的关系结构。然而，所得嵌入的几何结构仍然缺乏深入理解。本文表明，加权InfoNCE目标函数可以解释为距离几何问题，其中加权方案指定了表示需要实现的目标几何结构。这一视角为多个监督和弱监督目标的最优嵌入提供了精确刻画。在监督分类中，SupCon和Soft SupCon（一种密集松弛版本，其中来自不同类别的样本对具有较小的非零相似度）都会将每个类别内的样本坍缩到单一原型。然而，平衡的SupCon恢复了经典的正则单纯形几何，而类别不平衡打破了这种对称性：SupCon会根据类别大小产生非均匀的类间相似度，而Soft SupCon无论类别是否不平衡都能保持正则单纯形几何。在连续标签设置中，我们的框架揭示了另一种失败模式：y-Aware对比学习通常无法达到其熵最优，除非标签位于超球面上，这暴露了欧氏标签权重与球面潜在相似度之间的不匹配。相比之下，几何一致的选择（如欧氏-欧氏加权或X-CLR）能产生唯一的最优嵌入。我们的结果表明，加权方案的选择决定了对比学习在几何上是否可实现、退化或不一致，为设计对比学习目标提供了理论框架。

</details>


### [99] [Collider-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents with Particle Physics Analysis Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13950)
*Darius A. Faroughy,Sofia Palacios Schweitzer,Ian Pang,Siddharth Mishra-Sharma,David Shih*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Collider-Bench是一个评估LLM智能体在大型强子对撞机(LHC)实验分析再现任务中能力的基准。智能体需根据公开论文和科学软件，将已发表的分析转化为可执行的模拟-选择流水线，并提交预测结果。评估发现，目前没有通用编码智能体能可靠击败物理学家参与的解决方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准难以捕捉真实科学工作的复杂性和细微差别，特别是LHC实验分析因公开工具链与内部软件存在差异、论文省略实现细节而难以复现，因此需要专门的基准来评估LLM智能体在此类长周期工具使用任务中的表现。

Method: 构建Collider-Bench基准，包含来自LHC搜索的初始任务集、容器化沙箱和事件模拟工具；要求智能体将已发表分析转化为可执行的模拟-选择流水线，提交指定信号区间的碰撞事件产额预测；使用标准直方图指标进行连续保真度评分，并利用LLM评判器评估代码与完整会话轨迹以捕捉编造、幻觉和重复等失败模式。

Result: 在通用编码智能体的能力阶梯上评估，平均而言没有智能体能够可靠地击败物理学家参与的解决方案。

Conclusion: 目前通用编码智能体在复杂科学工作流再现任务上仍远不如人类专家参与的方法，Collider-Bench为相关研究提供了有价值的评估平台。

Original Abstract: Autonomous language-model agents are increasingly evaluated on long-horizon tool-use tasks, but existing benchmarks rarely capture the complexity and nuance of real scientific work. To address this gap, we introduce Collider-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating whether LLM agents can reproduce experimental analyses from the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) using only public papers and open scientific software. Such analyses are often difficult to reproduce because the public toolchain only approximates the software used internally by the experimental collaborations, while the published papers inevitably omit implementation details needed for a faithful reconstruction. Agents must therefore rely on physical reasoning, domain knowledge, and trial-and-error to fill these gaps. Each task requires the agent to turn a published analysis into an executable simulation-and-selection pipeline and submit predicted collision event yields in specified signal regions. These predictions are evaluated with standard histogram metrics that provide continuous fidelity scores without a hand-written rubric. We also report the computational cost incurred by each agent per task. Finally, we evaluate the codebase and full session trace using an LLM judge to catch qualitative failure modes such as fabrications, hallucinations and duplications. We release an initial set of tasks drawn from LHC searches, together with a containerized sandbox and event simulation tools. We evaluate across a capability ladder of general purpose coding agents. Our results show that on average no agent reliably beats the physicist-in-the-loop solution.

中文摘要翻译: 自主语言模型智能体越来越多地在长周期工具使用任务上被评估，但现有基准很少能够捕捉真实科学工作的复杂性和细微差别。为填补这一空白，我们提出了Collider-Bench，一个用于评估LLM智能体能否仅使用公开论文和开放科学软件再现大型强子对撞机(LHC)实验分析的基准。此类分析往往难以复现，因为公开工具链仅能近似实验合作组内部使用的软件，而已发表的论文不可避免地遗漏了忠实重建所需的实现细节。因此，智能体必须依赖物理推理、领域知识和试错来填补这些空白。每个任务要求智能体将已发表的分析转化为可执行的模拟与选择流水线，并在指定的信号区间内提交预测的碰撞事件产额。这些预测通过标准直方图指标进行评估，提供连续的保真度评分，无需手工编写的评分标准。我们还报告每个智能体在每个任务上产生的计算成本。最后，我们使用LLM评判器评估代码库和完整会话轨迹，以捕捉编造、幻觉和重复等定性失败模式。我们发布了一组初始任务，这些任务来自LHC搜索，并附有容器化沙箱和事件模拟工具。我们在通用编码智能体的能力阶梯上进行了评估。结果表明，平均而言，没有智能体能够可靠地击败物理学家参与的解决方案。

</details>


### [100] [REALM: Retrospective Encoder Alignment for LFP Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14867)
*Peicheng Wu,Zhenyu Bu,Runze Ma,Lin Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REALM框架，通过回顾式蒸馏将非因果的LFP解码模型转化为因果模型，实现实时神经解码，在性能提升的同时将参数量减少2倍、训练时间减少10倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尖峰信号虽时空分辨率高，但其高采样率导致高功耗和带宽需求，成为高通道数无线BCI的瓶颈。LFP虽具有长期稳定、低功耗和低带宽优势，但基于LFP的解码模型准确率较低且依赖非因果架构，无法用于实时部署。因此需要一种既能保持LFP优势又能实现实时因果解码的方法。

Method: 提出REALM回顾式蒸馏框架：1) 首先使用掩码自编码目标预训练一个双向Mamba-2教师模型；2) 然后通过表示对齐和任务监督的联合目标，将教师模型蒸馏为一个紧凑的学生因果模型，实现实时部署。

Result: REALM在行为解码任务上一致优于基于LFP的因果和非因果SOTA方法，参数量减少2倍，训练时间减少10倍，证明LFP模型无需依赖尖峰信号也能达到有竞争力的解码性能。

Conclusion: 回顾式蒸馏有效弥合了离线和实时神经解码之间的差距，REALM为下一代无线植入式BCI提供了实用且可扩展的LFP-only替代方案。

Original Abstract: Spike activity has been the dominant neural signal for behavior decoding due to its high spatial and temporal resolution. However, as brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) move toward high channel counts and wireless operation, the high sampling frequency of spike signals becomes a bottleneck due to high power and bandwidth requirements. Local field potentials (LFPs) represent a different spatial-temporal scale of brain activity compared to spikes, offering key advantages including improved long-term stability, reduced energy consumption, and lower bandwidth requirement. Despite these benefits, LFP-based decoding models typically show reduced accuracy and often rely on non-causal architectures that are unsuitable for real-time deployment. To address these challenges, we propose REALM: a retrospective distillation framework that enables causal LFP decoding. Inspired by offline-to-online distillation strategies in speech recognition, REALM transfers representational knowledge from a pretrained multi-session bidirectional LFP model to a causal version for real-time deployment. We first pretrain a bidirectional Mamba-2 teacher model using a masked autoencoding objective. We then distill this teacher model into a compact student model via a combined objective of representation alignment and task supervision. REALM consistently outperforms both causal and non-causal LFP-based SOTA methods for behavior decoding. Notably, our REALM improves decoding performance while achieving a $2\times$ reduction in parameter count and a $10\times$ reduction in training time. These results demonstrate that retrospective distillation effectively bridges the gap between offline and real-time neural decoding. REALM shows that LFP-only models can achieve competitive decoding performance without reliance on spike signals, offering a practical and scalable alternative for next-generation wireless implantable BCIs.

中文摘要翻译: 尖峰活动因其高时空分辨率一直被视为行为解码的主导神经信号。然而，随着脑机接口向高通道数和无线操作方向发展，尖峰信号的高采样频率因其高功率和带宽需求而成为瓶颈。局部场电位相较于尖峰信号代表了不同时空尺度的脑活动，具有长期稳定性好、能耗低和带宽需求小等关键优势。尽管有这些优点，基于LFP的解码模型通常准确率较低，且往往依赖于不适合实时部署的非因果架构。针对这些挑战，我们提出REALM：一种实现因果LFP解码的回顾式蒸馏框架。受语音识别中离线到在线蒸馏策略的启发，REALM将预训练的多会话双向LFP模型中的表征知识迁移到因果版本，以实现实时部署。我们首先使用掩码自编码目标预训练一个双向Mamba-2教师模型，然后通过表示对齐与任务监督的联合目标将该教师模型蒸馏为一个紧凑的学生模型。REALM在行为解码任务上一致优于基于LFP的因果和非因果最先进方法。值得注意的是，我们的REALM在提升解码性能的同时，实现了参数量减少2倍、训练时间减少10倍的效果。这些结果表明，回顾式蒸馏有效弥合了离线和实时神经解码之间的差距。REALM证明，仅基于LFP的模型无需依赖尖峰信号也能达到有竞争力的解码性能，为下一代无线植入式脑机接口提供了一种实用且可扩展的替代方案。

</details>


### [101] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WarmPrior，一种基于近期动作历史构造的时间感知先验分布，替代标准高斯分布作为生成策略的源分布，在机器人操作任务中持续提升成功率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前基于扩散和流匹配的生成式策略普遍使用标准高斯分布作为源分布，但该先验缺乏任务相关的时间结构信息，限制了生成路径的效率和策略性能。作者试图探索源分布这一被忽视的设计维度。

Method: WarmPrior：利用可获取的近期动作历史构造一个时间上具有根基性的先验分布，替代标准高斯分布作为扩散/流匹配模型的源分布；同时将其应用于先验空间强化学习以重塑探索分布。

Result: 1）在机器人操作任务上持续提升成功率；2）生成更直的路径（与Rectified Flow中最优传输耦合的效果类似）；3）在先验空间强化学习中同时提升样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 源分布是生成式机器人控制中一个重要但被低估的设计维度，WarmPrior通过提供更好的先验显著提升了生成策略的性能和效率。

Original Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

中文摘要翻译: 基于扩散和流匹配的生成式策略已成为视觉运动机器人控制的主流范式。我们发现，用WarmPrior——一种从可获取的近期动作历史中构建的简单时间根基先验——替代标准高斯源分布，能持续提升机器人操作任务的成功率。我们将这一改进归因于显著更直的路径，这与Rectified Flow中最优传输耦合的效果相呼应。超越标准行为克隆之外，WarmPrior还能重塑先验空间强化学习中的探索分布，同时提升样本效率和最终性能。综上，这些结果揭示了源分布是生成式机器人控制中一个重要但尚未被充分探索的设计轴。

</details>


### [102] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完整的能耗核算框架，用于量化蒸馏管道的端到端能耗（包括教师模型侧的数据生成、logit缓存和评估等被忽视的成本），并通过实验建立了能耗-质量帕累托前沿，为在能耗约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数提供了实用设计准则。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究在推广蒸馏技术时，往往只关注模型效率提升，忽略了蒸馏管道全流程（尤其是教师模型侧的数据生成、logit缓存和评估）的能耗与资源成本，缺乏系统的端到端能耗核算方法。

Method: 提出了一个完整的能耗核算框架，通过逐阶段跟踪GPU设备功耗来测量蒸馏管道的全计算成本；在实验中分离并记录了不同阶段的实际能耗，系统测量了两种常见蒸馏方法（基于logit的知识蒸馏和基于合成数据的监督微调）的能耗与排放，构建了能耗-质量帕累托前沿。

Result: 揭示了蒸馏过程中此前被忽视的成本，建立了能耗-质量帕累托前沿，并据此推导出在能耗和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的实用设计规则。

Conclusion: 发布了开源测量工具和核算协议，为可比较、可复现的蒸馏研究提供标准化基础，使管道全流程能耗影响得到明确核算。

Original Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模语言模型部署的增长推动了GPU需求和数据中心规模的激增，引发了关于电力使用、电网压力以及现代AI工作负载影响的担忧。蒸馏通常被视为获得更便宜、更高效模型的最有效路径之一，但这些说法很少考虑完整的端到端能耗和资源成本，包括关键的教师模型侧工作负载，如数据生成、logit缓存和评估。我们提出了一个完整的能耗核算框架，通过逐阶段详细跟踪GPU设备功耗来测量蒸馏管道的全计算成本。在我们的实验中，我们分离并记录了不同阶段的实际能耗，系统测量了两种常见蒸馏方法的能耗和排放：经典的基于logit的知识蒸馏和基于合成数据的监督微调，构建了能耗-质量帕累托前沿，揭示了此前被忽视的成本。基于这些测量和分析，我们推导出在能耗和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的实用设计规则，并发布了一个开源测量工具和核算协议，为可比较、可复现的蒸馏研究提供标准化基础，明确核算管道全流程的能耗影响。

</details>


### [103] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3是一个用于表格数据的预训练基础模型，通过仅使用合成数据预训练，在百万级数据规模、时间序列、关系型数据和表格文本数据上均取得SOTA性能，推理速度比TabPFN-2.5快20倍，且首次将测试时计算扩展引入表格基础模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 表格数据支撑着科学和工业中大多数高价值预测任务，TabPFN开创了这一模态的基础模型革命。为满足用户需求，需要将SOTA性能扩展到百万级训练样本、大幅减少训练和推理时间，并扩展到时间序列、关系型数据和表格文本数据等更多数据类型。

Method: TabPFN-3完全基于先验分布生成的合成数据进行预训练；引入测试时计算扩展（Test-time Compute Scaling），通过API产品TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking)实现；采用缩减的KV缓存和行分块（row-chunking）技术，实现在单块H100上扩展到100万行并保持快速推理。

Result: 在TabArena上以显著优势超越所有其他模型（包括调优和集成的基线），在速度/性能边界上占帕累托优势；在百万行200特征数据集上击败8小时调优的梯度提升树基线；TabPFN-3-Plus在TabArena上超越所有非TabPFN模型200+ Elo（最大数据子集上420 Elo），比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍；TabPFN-TS-3在fev-bench时间序列基准上排名第2；SHAP值计算加速高达120倍；整体比TabPFN-2.5快20倍。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3大幅推进了表格预测的前沿，在标准表格基准、时间序列、关系型数据和表格文本数据上均取得SOTA性能，同时实现了显著的加速，证明了纯合成数据预训练的基础模型在表格模态上的巨大潜力。

Original Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

中文摘要翻译: 表格数据支撑着科学和工业中大多数高价值预测问题，TabPFN引领了这一模态的基础模型革命。基于用户反馈设计的TabPFN-3在此基础上扩展，将最先进的性能扩展到具有100万训练行的数据集，并大幅减少训练和推断时间。TabPFN-3完全使用基于先验分布的合成数据进行预训练，极大地推进了表格预测的前沿，并在时间序列、关系型数据和表格文本数据上带来了显著提升。在标准表格基准TabArena上，TabPFN-3的单次前向传播以显著优势超越所有其他模型（包括调优和集成的基线），并在速度/性能边界上占据帕累托优势。在更多样化的数据集上，TabPFN-3在多类别数据集上排名第一，并在多达100万训练行和200个特征的数据集上击败了经过8小时调优的梯度提升树基线。TabPFN-3将测试时计算扩展引入表格基础模型。我们的API产品TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking)利用这一点，在TabArena上以超过200 Elo的优势击败所有非TabPFN模型，在最大数据子集上达到420 Elo的提升，并且在不使用大语言模型、真实数据、互联网搜索或任何其他模型（除TabPFN外）的情况下，比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍的同时表现更好。TabPFN-3扩展了我们模型的能力，实现了对关系型数据（在RelBenchV1上达到新的SOTA基础模型）和表格文本数据（通过TabPFN-3-Plus在TabSTAR上达到SOTA）的最先进预测；并改进了现有集成：一个专门检查点TabPFN-TS-3在时间序列基准fev-bench上排名第二，SHAP值计算加速高达120倍。TabPFN-3在实现这一性能的同时，速度比TabPFN-2.5快20倍。此外，缩减的KV缓存和行分块技术可在单块H100上扩展到100万行并保持快速推断速度。

</details>


### [104] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了离散扩散模型在去噪过程中存在层次结构：模型先学习数据的支撑结构（如语法有效性），再学习细粒度的频率信息。均匀扩散和吸收扩散在反向编辑中表现出不同的机制分离特性，实验验证了支撑定位早于频率排序的学习顺序。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管离散扩散模型在语言建模中越来越有竞争力，但其去噪目标如何组织学习过程仍不清晰。作者希望理解模型在学习数据分布时是否存在内在的层次结构，以及不同扩散机制（均匀/吸收）是否导致不同的学习行为。

Method: 理论证明在小噪声区域（最终去噪步骤）中，每个单token反向编辑可分解为领先尺度（决定是否向数据支撑移动）和精细系数（决定相同尺度内的相对概率）。分析了均匀扩散的三分结构（有效性改进/保持/恶化）和吸收扩散的领先阶质量集中在有效性改进移动上的特性。实验使用掩码语言扩散模型和合成正则语言任务验证理论预测。

Result: 理论证明了离散扩散模型反向过程中的尺度分离现象；均匀扩散呈现三分结构，吸收扩散将领先阶质量置于有效性改进移动上；实验表明支撑定位早于频率排序出现，且两种扩散机制的速率分离与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型在学习过程中遵循"先学习数据支撑，后学习数据频率"的层次顺序。不同的扩散机制（均匀/吸收）会导致不同的学习动态和速率分离特性。

Original Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are increasingly competitive for language modeling, yet it remains unclear how their denoising objectives organize learning. Although these objectives target the full data distribution, we show that the exact reverse process induces a hierarchy between coarse support information and finer frequency information. For uniform and absorbing (a.k.a. masking) diffusion, we prove that, in the small-noise regime of the final denoising steps, each single-token reverse edit decomposes into a leading scale, determined by whether it moves toward the data support (e.g., grammatically valid sentences), and a finer coefficient, determining relative probabilities within the same scale. Thus, recovering validity structure only requires learning the correct order of magnitude of reverse probabilities, whereas recovering data frequencies requires coefficient-level estimation. The separation is mechanism-dependent: uniform diffusion exhibits a trichotomy into validity-improving, validity-preserving, and validity-worsening edits, while absorbing diffusion places its leading-order mass on validity-improving moves. Experiments on a masked language diffusion model and synthetic regular-language tasks support these predictions: support-localization emerges earlier than within-support frequency ranking, and the contrast between uniform and absorbing diffusion matches the predicted rate separation. Together, our results suggest that discrete diffusion models learn data support before data frequencies.

中文摘要翻译: 离散扩散模型在语言建模中日益具有竞争力，但其去噪目标如何组织学习过程仍不明确。尽管这些目标针对完整的数据分布，我们证明精确的反向过程会在粗粒度的支撑信息与细粒度的频率信息之间引入层次结构。对于均匀扩散和吸收扩散（即掩码扩散），我们证明在小噪声区域（即最终去噪步骤）中，每个单token反向编辑可分解为一个主导尺度（由该编辑是否向数据支撑移动决定，例如语法有效的句子）和一个精细系数（决定同一尺度内的相对概率）。因此，恢复有效性结构只需学习反向概率的正确量级，而恢复数据频率则需要系数级别的估计。这种分离是机制依赖的：均匀扩散呈现三分结构——有效性改进、有效性保持和有效性恶化三类编辑，而吸收扩散将其领先阶质量置于有效性改进移动上。在掩码语言扩散模型和合成正则语言任务上的实验支持这些预测：支撑定位的出现早于支撑内的频率排序，且均匀扩散与吸收扩散之间的对比与预测的速率分离相匹配。综上，我们的结果表明离散扩散模型在数据频率之前学习数据支撑。

</details>


### [105] [Dywave: Event-Aligned Dynamic Tokenization for Heterogeneous IoT Sensing Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14014)
*Tomoyoshi Kimura,Denizhan Kara,Jinyang Li,Hongjue Zhao,Yigong Hu,Yizhuo Chen,Xiaomin Ouyang,Shengzhong Liu,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dywave提出了一种面向物联网感知信号的动态分词框架，利用小波层次分解识别语义事件边界，自适应压缩冗余区间，在五个真实数据集上相比SOTA方法准确率提升12%，输入长度减少75%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 物联网信号具有非平稳、多尺度特性，标准分词技术难以捕捉其内在时序结构与物理事件边界，导致输入表示冗余且缺乏语义对齐。

Method: 基于小波层次分解，识别与底层语义事件对应的时序边界，自适应压缩冗余区间，同时保持时序连贯性，构建紧凑的输入表示。

Result: 在五个真实物联网感知数据集（活动识别、压力评估、附近物体检测）上，准确率较SOTA方法提升最高12%，主流序列模型输入token长度减少75%，且对域偏移和变长序列具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Dywave动态分词框架能有效对齐物联网信号的内在语义结构，在提升准确率的同时大幅降低计算开销，并增强了模型的泛化鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems continuously collect heterogeneous sensing signals from ubiquitous sensors to support intelligent applications such as human activity analysis, emotion monitoring, and environmental perception. These signals are inherently non-stationary and multi-scale, posing unique challenges for standard tokenization techniques. This paper proposes Dywave, a dynamic tokenization framework for IoT sensing signals that constructs compact input representations aligned with intrinsic temporal structures and underlying physical events. Dywave leverages wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition, identifies meaningful temporal boundaries corresponding to underlying semantic events, and adaptively compresses redundant intervals while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive evaluations on five real-world IoT sensing datasets across activity recognition, stress assessment, and nearby object detection demonstrate that Dywave outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 12% in accuracy, while improving computational efficiency by reducing input token lengths by up to 75% across mainstream sequence models. Moreover, Dywave exhibits improved robustness to domain shifts and varying sequence lengths.

中文摘要翻译: 物联网系统持续从无处不在的传感器中收集异构感知信号，以支持人类活动分析、情绪监测和环境感知等智能应用。这些信号本质上是非平稳且多尺度的，给标准分词技术带来了独特挑战。本文提出Dywave，一种面向物联网感知信号的动态分词框架，能够构建与内在时序结构和底层物理事件对齐的紧凑输入表示。Dywave利用基于小波的层次分解，识别与底层语义事件对应的有意义的时序边界，并自适应压缩冗余区间，同时保持时序连贯性。在五个真实物联网感知数据集上的广泛评估表明，在活动识别、压力评估和附近物体检测任务中，Dywave在准确率上比最先进方法提升高达12%，同时通过将主流序列模型的输入token长度减少高达75%来提高计算效率。此外，Dywave对域偏移和变长序列表现出更强的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [106] [R2R2: Robust Representation for Intensive Experience Reuse via Redundancy Reduction in Self-Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14026)
*Sanghyeob Song,Donghyeok Lee,Jinsik Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对数据稀缺场景下强化学习中高更新-数据比（UTD）导致的自预测学习（SPL）表征过拟合问题，提出冗余减少的鲁棒表征（R²R²）正则化方法，通过理论分析发现标准零中心化与SPL谱特性冲突并设计非中心化目标，实验证明在11个连续控制任务上有效缓解过拟合，在UTD=20时提升TD7约22%，并进一步改进了最新方法SimbaV2-SPL。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工作主要关注评论员偏差，而高更新-数据比（UTD）条件下自预测学习（SPL）中表征层面的不稳定性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出冗余减少的鲁棒表征（R²R²）方法，理论上识别标准零中心化与SPL谱特性的冲突，设计非中心化目标作为SPL内的正则化方法。

Result: 在11个连续控制任务上，UTD=20时R²R²将TD7提升约22%；R²R²在SimbaV2-SPL上提供额外增益，SimbaV2-SPL自身即达到新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: R²R²有效缓解高UTD下的表征过拟合，且与现有进展正交，可进一步提升已有方法性能。

Original Abstract: For reinforcement learning in data-scarce domains like real-world robotics, intensive data reuse enhances efficiency but induces overfitting. While prior works focus on critic bias, representation-level instability in Self-Predictive Learning (SPL) under high Update-to-Data (UTD) regimes remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction (R2R2), a regularization method within SPL. We theoretically identify that standard zero-centering conflicts with SPL's spectral properties and design a non-centered objective accordingly. We verify R2R2 on SPL-native algorithms like TD7. Furthermore, to demonstrate its orthogonality to prior advancements, we extend the state-of-the-art SimbaV2, which originally lacks SPL, by integrating a tailored SPL module, termed SimbaV2-SPL. Experiments across 11 continuous control tasks confirm that R2R2 effectively mitigates overfitting; specifically, at a UTD ratio of 20, it improves TD7 by ~22% and provides additional gains on top of SimbaV2-SPL, which itself establishes a new state-of-the-art. The code can be found at: https://github.com/songsang7/R2R2

中文摘要翻译: 对于像真实机器人这样的数据稀缺领域中的强化学习，密集的数据重利用提高了效率但导致了过拟合。虽然先前的工作主要关注评论员偏差，但在高更新-数据比（UTD）设定下，自预测学习（SPL）中表征层面的不稳定性仍未得到充分探索。为填补这一空白，我们提出了冗余减少的鲁棒表征（R²R²），一种在SPL中的正则化方法。我们从理论上识别出标准的零中心化与SPL的谱特性相冲突，并据此设计了一个非中心化目标。我们在SPL原生算法（如TD7）上验证了R²R²。此外，为了展示其与先前进展的正交性，我们将原本缺乏SPL的最先进方法SimbaV2通过整合一个定制的SPL模块进行扩展，称为SimbaV2-SPL。在11个连续控制任务上的实验证实，R²R²有效缓解了过拟合；具体而言，在UTD比为20时，它将TD7提升了约22%，并在SimbaV2-SPL（其本身即建立了新的最先进水平）的基础上提供了额外增益。代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/songsang7/R2R2

</details>


### [107] [Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention: Learning When to Write by Predicting Future Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14037)
*Gergely Szilvasy,Manuel Faysse,Maria Lomeli,Matthijs Douze,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Loïc Cabannes,Wen-tau Yih,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SP-KV机制，通过轻量级效用预测器对KV对进行评分，动态决定哪些旧KV对保留在缓存中，实现3-10倍的KV缓存压缩，且几乎不损失性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在测试时计算和智能体范式中，语言模型处理越来越长的序列，Transformer架构的文本生成效率受限于Key-Value缓存的内存占用和带宽瓶颈。

Method: SP-KV（自剪枝键值注意力）机制：训练一个轻量级效用预测器为每个KV对打分，近期KV始终通过局部窗口可用，旧KV对仅在预测效用超过阈值时才写入缓存并用于全局注意力。LLM与效用预测器通过下一标记预测损失进行端到端联合训练，从预训练LLM检查点适配而来。

Result: SP-KV实现动态稀疏化，KV缓存大小压缩3-10倍，长序列压缩比更高；显著提升内存使用和解码速度，验证损失和下游任务性能几乎无下降；揭示了可用于指导混合局部-全局注意力架构设计的结构化层和头特异性稀疏模式。

Conclusion: SP-KV是一种有效的KV缓存压缩机制，不仅能大幅减少内存占用和加速解码，还能揭示注意力头部的结构化稀疏模式，为设计更高效的混合注意力架构提供指导。

Original Abstract: Under modern test-time compute and agentic paradigms, language models process ever-longer sequences. Efficient text generation with transformer architectures is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value cache memory footprint and bandwidth. To address this limitation, we introduce Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV), a mechanism designed to predict future KV utility in order to reduce the size of the long-term KV cache. This strategy operates at a fine granularity: a lightweight utility predictor scores each key-value pair, and while recent KVs are always available via a local window, older pairs are written in the cache and used in global attention only if their predicted utility surpasses a given threshold. The LLM and the utility predictor are trained jointly end-to-end exclusively through next-token prediction loss, and are adapted from pretrained LLM checkpoints.
  Rather than enforcing a fixed compression ratio, SP-KV performs dynamic sparsification: the mechanism adapts to the input and typically reduces the KV cache size by a factor of $3$ to $10\times$, longer sequences often being more compressible. This leads to vast improvements in memory usage and decoding speed, with little to no degradation of validation loss nor performance on a broad set of downstream tasks. Beyond serving as an effective KV-cache reduction mechanism, our method reveals structured layer- and head-specific sparsity patterns that we can use to guide the design of hybrid local-global attention architectures.

中文摘要翻译: 在现代测试时计算和智能体范式下，语言模型处理的序列越来越长。基于Transformer架构的高效文本生成日益受到键值缓存内存占用和带宽的制约。为解决这一限制，我们提出了自剪枝键值注意力（Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention, SP-KV），这是一种旨在预测未来KV效用以减少长期KV缓存大小的机制。该策略在细粒度上运行：一个轻量级效用预测器为每个键值对评分，最近期的KV始终通过局部窗口可用，而较旧的KV对被写入缓存并仅在预测效用超过给定阈值时才用于全局注意力。LLM和效用预测器通过仅依赖下一标记预测损失进行端到端联合训练，并从预训练的LLM检查点适配而来。SP-KV不强制执行固定的压缩比，而是执行动态稀疏化：该机制自适应地针对输入进行调整，通常将KV缓存大小减少3到10倍，较长的序列往往具有更高的可压缩性。这带来了内存使用和解码速度的巨大改进，同时在广泛的 downstream 任务上几乎没有验证损失或性能退化。除了作为一种有效的KV缓存缩减机制外，我们的方法揭示了结构化的层特异性和头特异性稀疏模式，这些模式可用于指导混合局部-全局注意力架构的设计。

</details>


### [108] [Reliability-Gated Source Anchoring for Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14063)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Weicong Chen,Sabyasachi Sahoo,Sreehari Sankar,Biyao Zhang,Mohsen Harir,Shouren Wang,Osama Zafar,Christian Gagné,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RMemSafe，一种可靠性门控机制，通过检测冻结源模型的预测熵来动态减弱不可靠源锚点的约束，解决持续测试时适应（CTTA）中源模型退化时的"盲锚定"问题，在CCC-Hard等极端域偏移场景下显著提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有源锚定CTTA方法在源模型严重退化时（如ResNet-50在CCC-Hard上准确率降至约1.3%）仍以恒定强度锚定源模型，导致"盲锚定"失效。需要一种能感知源模型可靠性并动态调整锚定强度的机制。

Method: RMemSafe扩展自ROID方法，利用冻结源模型的归一化预测熵作为可靠性门控信号。当源后验分布接近均匀分布（高熵）时，门控关闭，消除源锚定和一致性滤波，将目标简化为源无关的回退损失（ROID基础损失+边际校准）。通过归一化预测熵动态衰减目标中所有显式源耦合项。

Result: 在9个匹配分割的持续损坏单元中，RMemSafe+ASR在8个上取得最低错误率，在所有9个上是最优的重置方法。相比ROID+ASR，ResNet-50提升1.05个百分点，ViT-B/16提升0.48个百分点。源退化扫描实验中，RMemSafe的损伤斜率比ROID+ASR浅1.13倍，符合"优雅退化"的预期。

Conclusion: 熵门控机制能有效检测源模型的"高熵崩溃"（而非置信度错误的低熵源），使得CTTA方法在源模型不可靠时能自动降级为源无关的鲁棒回退策略，实现源模型退化场景下的优雅退化。

Original Abstract: Continual test-time adaptation (CTTA) updates a pretrained model online on an unlabeled, non-stationary stream while anchoring it to a frozen source checkpoint. This anchor is useful only when the source remains reliable. On CCC-Hard, however, a ResNet-50 source falls to approximately $1.3\%$ top-$1$ accuracy, while existing source-anchored CTTA methods continue applying the same anchor strength. We call this failure mode blind anchoring and propose RMemSafe, a reliability-gated extension of ROID that uses the frozen source's normalized predictive entropy to attenuate all explicit source-coupled uses in the objective. When the source posterior approaches uniformity, the gate closes: the source anchor and agreement filter vanish, and the objective reduces to a source-agnostic fallback comprising ROID's base losses plus marginal calibration. Combined with ASR, RMemSafe achieves the lowest error on $8$ of $9$ matched-split continual-corruption cells and is the best reset-based method on all $9$, improving ROID+ASR by $1.05$~pp on ResNet-50 and $0.48$~pp on ViT-B/16. A controlled source-degradation sweep shows a $1.13{\times}$ shallower harm slope than ROID+ASR, consistent with the graceful-decay prediction. The entropy gate detects high-entropy source collapse, not confidently wrong low-entropy sources; this scope is explicitly evaluated and discussed.

中文摘要翻译: 持续测试时适应（CTTA）在线更新预训练模型于无标签的非平稳数据流，同时将其锚定至冻结的源检查点。该锚点在源模型保持可靠时才有用。然而在CCC-Hard上，ResNet-50源模型的top-1准确率降至约1.3%，而现有源锚定CTTA方法仍以恒定强度施加锚定。我们将这种失效模式称为"盲锚定"，并提出RMemSafe——一种ROID的可靠性门控扩展，利用冻结源模型的归一化预测熵来衰减目标中所有显式源耦合项。当源后验分布趋近均匀分布时，门控关闭：源锚定和一致性滤波消失，目标简化为由ROID基础损失加边际校准组成的源无关回退方案。结合ASR后，RMemSafe在9个匹配分割的持续损坏单元中的8个上取得了最低错误率，并且在全部9个上是最优的重置方法，在ResNet-50上比ROID+ASR提升了1.05个百分点，在ViT-B/16上提升了0.48个百分点。受控的源退化扫描实验显示，RMemSafe的损伤斜率比ROID+ASR浅1.13倍，与"优雅退化"的预测一致。熵门控检测的是高熵源崩溃，而非置信度错误的低熵源；该适用范围已得到明确评估与讨论。

</details>


### [109] [SurF: A Generative Model for Multivariate Irregular Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14069)
*Mohammad R. Rezaei,Tejas Balaji,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SurF模型，利用时间重标度定理作为可学习双射，对不规则采样多元事件流进行生成建模，在多个基准上达到最优，迈出异步事件流基础模型的第一步。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法面临两大瓶颈：基于token化的方法在处理变化幅度达数量级的非均匀间隔事件时失效；神经时序点过程受限于窗口级数值积分，难以高效建模。

Method: (i) 提出SurF模型，将时间重标度定理（TRT）作为事件序列与i.i.d.单位速率指数噪声之间的可学习双射；(ii) 设计三种高效参数化累积强度函数，可扩展至长序列；(iii) 采用基于Transformer的编码器实现多数据集预训练。

Result: 在6个真实基准上，SurF在Earthquake、Retweet和Taobao数据集上取得最佳时间RMSE；在严格的留一法评估协议下，存留检查点在5/6的数据集上击败所有经典和神经自回归基线，在Amazon和Earthquake上击败所有基线。

Conclusion: SurF是迈向异步事件流基础模型的第一步，通过统一的生成框架实现跨异构数据集的训练和迁移。

Original Abstract: Irregularly sampled multivariate event streams remain a stubbornly difficult modality for generative modeling: tokenization-based approaches break down when inter-event intervals vary by orders of magnitude, and neural temporal point processes are bottlenecked by window-level numerical quadrature. We (i) propose SurF, a generative model that uses the Time Rescaling Theorem (TRT) as a learnable bijection between event sequences and i.i.d.\ unit-rate exponential noise, enabling a single model to be trained across heterogeneous event-stream datasets; (ii) three efficient parameterizations of the cumulative intensity that scale to long sequences; and (iii) a Transformer-based encoder for multi-dataset pretraining. On six real-world benchmarks, SurF achieves the best reported time RMSE on Earthquake, Retweet, and Taobao, and is within trial-level noise of the strongest specialist on the remaining three. Under a strict leave-one-out protocol, the held-out checkpoint beats every classical and neural-autoregressive baseline on 5/6 datasets and beats every baseline on Amazon and Earthquake, an initial step toward foundation models over asynchronous event streams.

中文摘要翻译: 不规则采样的多元事件流仍然是生成建模中一个难以处理的模态：基于分词的方法在事件间隔变化幅度达数量级时失效，而神经时序点过程受限于窗口级数值积分瓶颈。我们提出：(i) SurF，一种生成模型，利用时间重标度定理作为事件序列与独立同分布单位速率指数噪声之间的可学习双射，使单一模型能够在异构事件流数据集上训练；(ii) 三种高效的累积强度参数化方案，可扩展至长序列；(iii) 基于Transformer的编码器，用于多数据集预训练。在六个真实世界基准上，SurF在Earthquake、Retweet和Taobao数据集上取得了最佳报告的时间RMSE，在其余三个数据集上其结果落在最优专有模型的一次试验噪声范围内。在严格的留一法评估协议下，存留检查点在5/6的数据集上击败了所有经典和神经自回归基线，并在Amazon和Earthquake上击败了所有基线——这是迈向异步事件流基础模型的第一步。

</details>


### [110] [Fair and Calibrated Toxicity Detection with Robust Training and Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14074)
*Mokshit Surana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了毒性分类中的公平性问题，发现校准偏差是隐蔽的不公平现象，训练时干预措施只是重新分配而非消除偏差，后验方法会继承训练阶段的失败模式，且弃权机制本身也存在不公平性，主张公平性评估需要多轴框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究孤立地评估训练时干预和后验安全机制，忽略了二者之间的依赖关系；同时，公平性评估通常只关注排序指标（如AUC），忽视了校准和弃权维度，导致隐蔽的不公平现象被掩盖。

Method: 对比了经验风险最小化（ERM）、实例级重加权和Group DRO三种训练方法，结合温度缩放、基于置信度的弃权和每身份阈值优化，使用子组AUC、BPSN/BNSP AUC、误差差距和子组期望校准误差（ECE）进行多轴评估。

Result: (1) ERM整体校准良好（ECE 0.013）但各身份子组校准偏差显著（+0.029至+0.134）；(2) 重加权ERM提升排序但恶化校准公平性差距（最多+0.232），Group DRO消除校准偏差但全局校准变差（ECE 0.118）；(3) 温度缩放因非均匀校准而失效，基于置信度的弃权在ERM下有效但在DRO下失效；(4) 弃权本身对背景内容比对身份提及内容更有利。

Conclusion: 毒性分类的公平性需要同时考虑排序、校准和弃权三个维度，仅关注聚合排序指标的方法可能掩盖导致实际危害的失败模式差异，建议采用多轴公平性框架。

Original Abstract: Fairness in toxicity classification involves three integrated axes: ranking, calibration, and abstention. Training-time interventions and post-hoc safety mechanisms cannot be evaluated independently because the former determines the efficacy of the latter. We compare Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM), instance-level reweighting, and Group DRO across these axes, combined with temperature scaling, confidence-based abstention, and per-identity threshold optimization. Evaluation uses subgroup AUC, BPSN/BNSP AUC, error gaps, and per-subgroup Expected Calibration Error (ECE) with bootstrap CIs ($n = 1000$).
  We report four findings. (1) Calibration disparity is a hidden fairness violation. ERM has near-perfect aggregate calibration ($0.013$) but is significantly miscalibrated across all identity subgroups ($+0.029$ to $+0.134$). (2) Training interventions reshape rather than eliminate disparity. Reweighted ERM improves ranking (BPSN AUC $+0.06$ to $+0.12$) but worsens the calibration-fairness gap by up to $+0.232$. Group DRO eliminates calibration disparity but only by becoming uniformly miscalibrated globally (ECE $0.118$). (3) Post-hoc methods inherit training failure modes. Temperature scaling fails because miscalibration is non-uniform. Confidence-based abstention works under ERM but breaks under DRO, where the risk-coverage curve rises with deferral. (4) Abstention itself is unfair. Confidence-based deferral helps background content far more than identity-mentioning content. We argue that SRAI fairness requires a multi-axis framework: methods that differ only in aggregate ranking can differ sharply in failure modes that determine real-world harm.

中文摘要翻译: 毒性分类中的公平性涉及三个相互关联的轴：排序、校准和弃权。训练时干预措施与后验安全机制不能独立评估，因为前者决定了后者的有效性。我们比较了经验风险最小化（ERM）、实例级重加权和Group DRO在这三个轴上的表现，并结合温度缩放、基于置信度的弃权和每身份阈值优化。评估使用子组AUC、BPSN/BNSP AUC、误差差距和带自举置信区间（n=1000）的子组期望校准误差（ECE）。我们报告四项发现。(1) 校准差异是一种隐蔽的公平性违反。ERM具有近乎完美的聚合校准（0.013），但在所有身份子组上显著校准偏差（+0.029至+0.134）。(2) 训练干预措施重新分配而非消除差异。重加权ERM改进了排序（BPSN AUC +0.06至+0.12），但将校准-公平性差距恶化了最多+0.232。Group DRO消除了校准差异，但代价是全局校准均匀变差（ECE 0.118）。(3) 后验方法继承了训练阶段的失败模式。温度缩放因非均匀校准而失效。基于置信度的弃权在ERM下有效，但在DRO下失效，其风险-覆盖率曲线随 deferral 上升。(4) 弃权本身是不公平的。基于置信度的 deferral 对背景内容的帮助远大于对身份提及内容。我们认为SRAI公平性需要一个多轴框架：仅在聚合排序上不同的方法可能在决定实际危害的失败模式上存在显著差异。

</details>


### [111] [GenAI for Energy-Efficient and Interference-Aware Compressed Sensing of GNSS Signals on a Google Edge TPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14839)
*Thorben Wegner,Lucas Heublein,Tobias Feigl,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Alexander Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成式人工智能（GenAI）和变分自编码器（VAE）的硬件端方法，在Google Edge TPU上实时压缩和分类GNSS干扰/欺骗信号，实现超42倍压缩且对约72种干扰类型的分类F2分数达0.915，几乎不损失原始信号分类性能（0.923）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统GNSS干扰信号分类方法依赖原始或频谱数据流后处理，需要复杂且高成本的云数据传输；同时，GNSS干扰日益普遍，亟需适用于功耗受限环境的实时解决方案。

Method: 采用变分自编码器（VAE）作为生成式AI模型，直接在Google Edge TPU上对GNSS数据流进行压缩和干扰分类。通过8位量化适配大规模自编码器模型以实现能效部署。评估多种自编码器架构（包括条件VAE和FactorVAE），在原始IQ数据、FFT数据和手工特征上进行实验。

Result: 系统实现超42倍的数据压缩，对约72种干扰类型的分类F2分数为0.915，与原始信号的0.923非常接近。消融实验探索了条件VAE和FactorVAE的潜在特征解缠，提升了模型可解释性。

Conclusion: 该硬件为中心的GenAI方法显著降低了干扰信号传输成本，为功耗受限环境下的GNSS干扰缓解提供了实用解决方案，同时通过特征解缠增强了ML模型在敏感干扰应用中的可信度。

Original Abstract: Traditional methods for classifying global navigation satellite system (GNSS) jamming signals typically involve post-processing raw or spectral data streams, requiring complex and costly data transmission to cloud-based interference classification systems. In contrast, our proposed approach efficiently compresses GNSS data streams directly at the hardware receiver while simultaneously classifying jamming and spoofing attacks in real time. Given the growing prevalence of GNSS jamming, there is a critical need for real-time solutions suitable for power-constrained environments. This paper introduces a novel method for compressing and classifying GNSS jamming threats using generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), specifically variational autoencoders (VAEs), deployed on Google Edge tensor processing units (TPUs). The study evaluates various autoencoder (AE) architectures to compress and reconstruct GNSS signals, focusing on preserving interference characteristics while minimizing data size near the receiver hardware. The pipeline adapts large-scale AE models for Google Edge TPUs through 8-bit quantization to ensure energy-efficient deployment. Tests on raw in-phase and quadrature-phase (IQ) data, Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) data, and handcrafted features show the system achieves significant compression (>42x) and accurate classification of approximately 72 interference types on reconstructed signals (F2-score 0.915), closely matching the original signals (F2-score 0.923). The hardware-centric GenAI approach also substantially reduces jammer signal transmission costs, offering a practical solution for interference mitigation. Ablation studies on conditional and factorized VAEs (i.e., FactorVAE) explore latent feature disentanglement for data generation, enhancing model interpretability and fostering trust in machine learning (ML) solutions for sensitive interference applications.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的全球导航卫星系统（GNSS）干扰信号分类方法通常涉及对原始或频谱数据流进行后处理，需要将数据传送到基于云的干扰分类系统，过程复杂且成本高昂。相比之下，我们提出的方法在硬件接收机端直接高效压缩GNSS数据流，同时实时分类干扰和欺骗攻击。鉴于GNSS干扰日益普遍，亟需适用于功耗受限环境的实时解决方案。本文介绍了一种新颖的方法，利用生成式人工智能（GenAI），特别是变分自编码器（VAEs），部署在Google Edge张量处理单元（TPUs）上，对GNSS干扰威胁进行压缩和分类。本研究评估了多种自编码器（AE）架构以压缩和重建GNSS信号，重点关注在接收机硬件附近最小化数据尺寸的同时保留干扰特征。该流水线通过8位量化将大规模自编码器模型适配到Google Edge TPU，确保能效部署。在原始同相与正交相位（IQ）数据、快速傅里叶变换（FFT）数据以及手工特征上的测试表明，系统实现了显著的压缩率（>42倍），并对重建信号上约72种干扰类型进行了准确分类（F2分数0.915），与原始信号的分类性能（F2分数0.923）高度接近。这种以硬件为中心的GenAI方法还大幅降低了干扰信号传输成本，为干扰缓解提供了实用解决方案。针对条件VAE和因子化VAE（即FactorVAE）的消融研究探索了用于数据生成的潜在特征解缠，增强了模型的可解释性，并提升了机器学习（ML）解决方案在敏感干扰应用中的可信度。

</details>


### [112] [Not All Symbols Are Equal: Importance-Aware Constellation Design for Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14940)
*Albert Shaju,Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Mayukh Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种联合语义-物理层框架，通过向量量化变分自编码器提取离散语义概念、语义关键性指标（SCI）评分、深度强化学习动态选择传输子集，以及语义感知的M-QAM星座设计，实现了任务关键信息的高效保护，在4-QAM到1024-QAM上语义保护概率接近100%，压缩比达21:1。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有语义通信系统将星座设计与语义编码解耦，导致关键符号与无关符号以相同速率暴露于信道错误中，未能为任务相关信息提供差异化保护。

Method: 1) 向量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）提取离散潜在语义概念；2) 语义关键性指标（SCI）按任务相关性评分每个语义概念；3) 深度强化学习根据即时信道条件动态选择传输子集；4) 语义感知的M-QAM星座，根据联合共现统计量和SCI分数分配符号位置，替代标准均匀间距和格雷编码。

Result: 所提星座在4-QAM到1024-QAM上语义保护概率（SPP）接近100%，而标准星座在高频谱效率下仅约50%；压缩比达21:1且语义质量高于0.9；在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和FSDD上无需修改即可泛化。

Conclusion: 证明了任何格雷编码星座在非均匀语义重要性和共现统计量下，在SCI加权的语义符号脆弱性（SSV）指标上严格次优；所提联合语义-物理层框架显著提升任务关键信息的传输可靠性。

Original Abstract: Semantic communication systems for goal-oriented transmission must protect task-relevant information not only through source compression but also via physical layer mapping. Existing approaches decouple constellation design and semantic encoding, exposing critical symbols to channel errors at the same rate as irrelevant ones. Contrary to this, in this paper, a joint semantic-physical layer framework is proposed, which is composed of a vector quantized-variational autoencoder that extracts discrete latent concepts, a semantic criticality indicator (SCI) that scores each concept by task relevance, and a deep reinforcement learning agent that dynamically selects the transmission subset based on instantaneous channel conditions. At the physical layer, a learned semantic-aware M -QAM constellation assigns symbol positions according to joint co-occurrence statistics and SCI scores, departing from the uniform spacing and Gray coding of standard M -QAM which minimizes average BER without regard for semantic content. We introduce a novel semantic symbol vulnerability (SSV) metric and a semantic protection probability (SPP) to quantify the exposure of task-critical symbols to decoding errors, and prove that any Gray-coded constellation is strictly suboptimal in SCI-Weighted SSV whenever the source exhibits non-uniform semantic importance and co-occurrence statistics. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed constellation achieves near 100% SPP across modulation orders from 4-QAM to 1024-QAM versus 50% for standard constellations at high spectral efficiency, a 21:1 compression ratio with semantic quality above 0.9, generalizing across MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and FSDD without modification.

中文摘要翻译: 面向目标传输的语义通信系统不仅需通过源压缩保护任务相关信息，还需通过物理层映射实现保护。现有方法将星座设计与语义编码解耦，导致关键符号与无关符号以相同速率暴露于信道错误。与此相反，本文提出了一种联合语义-物理层框架，该框架由向量量化变分自编码器（用于提取离散潜在语义概念）、语义关键性指标（SCI，按任务相关性对每个概念评分）以及深度强化学习智能体（根据即时信道条件动态选择传输子集）组成。在物理层，一种学习到的语义感知M-QAM星座根据联合共现统计量和SCI分数分配符号位置，突破了标准M-QAM的均匀间距和格雷编码（后者在最小化平均误比特率时未考虑语义内容）。我们引入了新型语义符号脆弱性（SSV）指标和语义保护概率（SPP）来量化任务关键符号暴露于解码错误的风险，并证明了当源数据呈现非均匀语义重要性和共现统计量时，任何格雷编码星座在SCI加权的SSV上都是严格次优的。仿真结果表明，所提星座在4-QAM到1024-QAM调制阶数上实现了接近100%的SPP，而标准星座在高频谱效率下仅约50%；压缩比达21:1且语义质量高于0.9，且在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和FSDD数据集上无需修改即可泛化。

</details>


### [113] [bde: A Python Package for Bayesian Deep Ensembles via MILE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14146)
*Vyron Arvanitis,Angelos Aslanidis,Emanuel Sommer,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: bde是一个基于JAX实现的Python包，专注于表格数据的贝叶斯深度集成，提供高效的采样推理和不确定性量化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 为表格数据提供用户友好的贝叶斯深度集成工具，降低使用门槛，提升训练和不确定性量化的效率。

Method: 基于JAX高效的微正则朗之万集成（MILE）采样推理方法，提供与scikit-learn兼容的估计器，支持快速训练和高效MCMC采样。

Result: 在回归和分类任务上实现了快速训练、高效马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛采样以及不确定性量化。

Conclusion: bde包为表格数据的贝叶斯深度集成提供了高效、易用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: bde is a user-friendly Python package for Bayesian Deep Ensembles with a particular focus on tabular data. Built on an efficient JAX implementation of the sampling-based inference method Microcanonical Langevin Ensembles (MILE), it provides scikit-learn compatible estimators for fast training, efficient Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampling, and uncertainty quantification in both regression and classification tasks.

中文摘要翻译: bde是一个用户友好的Python包，专注于表格数据的贝叶斯深度集成。它基于高效的JAX实现的基于采样的推理方法——微正则朗之万集成（MILE），提供了与scikit-learn兼容的估计器，支持快速训练、高效马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛采样，以及回归和分类任务中的不确定性量化。

</details>


### [114] [Uncovering Trajectory and Topological Signatures in Multimodal Pediatric Sleep Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14156)
*Scott Ye,Harlin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索了多模态掩码自编码器生成的儿科PSG嵌入中的隐式结构，发现基于PHATE的几何特征、持续同调的拓扑特征以及EHR临床特征能为睡眠分析提供互补且可解释的信号，显著提升分类校准性和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管生成模型在儿科睡眠分析中展现出潜力，但其多模态嵌入的隐式结构仍不明确，本文旨在探究这些嵌入中蕴含的会话级诊断信息，并验证几何、拓扑及临床特征是否能提供互补的、可解释的判别信号。

Method: 使用多模态掩码自编码器提取30秒儿科PSG片段的嵌入，然后通过PHATE降维获取每历元几何坐标，利用持续同调提取整个嵌入云的拓扑摘要，并结合EHR临床特征。采用简单的线性模型和MLP模型（而非追求最先进性能）进行融合分类，以保持可解释性。

Result: 在四项二元预测任务中，晚期融合模型性能最优：去饱和AUPRC从0.26提升至0.34，EEG觉醒从0.31提升至0.48，低通气从0.09提升至0.22，呼吸暂停从0.05提升至0.14。完整融合模型在所有任务中取得最佳校准（最低Brier分数和期望校准误差）。

Conclusion: 潜在几何/拓扑结构与EHR能够提供嵌入本身之外互补且可解释的信号，在极端类别不平衡条件下显著提升模型的校准性和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: While generative models have shown promise in pediatric sleep analysis, the latent structure of their multimodal embeddings remains poorly understood. This work investigates session-wide diagnostic information contained in the sequences of 30-second pediatric PSG epochs embedded by a multimodal masked autoencoder. We test whether augmenting embeddings with PHATE-derived per-epoch coordinates and whole-night movement descriptors, persistent homology summaries of the embedding cloud, and EHR yields task-relevant signals. Simple linear and MLP models, chosen for interpretability rather than state-of-the-art performance, show that geometric, topological, and clinical features each provide complementary gains. For binary predictions, feature importance is task-dependent, and more expressive late-fusion models generally perform better, with AUPRC improving from 0.26 to 0.34 for desaturation, 0.31 to 0.48 for EEG arousal, 0.09 to 0.22 for hypopnea, and 0.05 to 0.14 for apnea. We also report Brier score and Expected Calibration Error, where the full fusion model yields the best calibration across all four binary tasks. Our study reveals that latent geometry/topology and EHR offer complementary, interpretable signals beyond embeddings, improving calibration and robustness under extreme imbalance.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管生成模型在儿科睡眠分析中展现出潜力，但其多模态嵌入的隐式结构仍未被充分理解。本研究探究了由多模态掩码自编码器编码的30秒儿科PSG历元序列中蕴含的会话级诊断信息。我们测试了将嵌入与PHATE导出的每历元坐标、整夜运动描述子、嵌入云的持续同调摘要以及EHR相结合是否能产生任务相关信号。采用线性模型和MLP模型（选择依据是可解释性而非最先进性能）表明，几何特征、拓扑特征和临床特征各自提供了互补的增益。在二元预测任务中，特征重要性依赖于具体任务，更具表达力的晚期融合模型通常表现更优：去饱和AUPRC从0.26提升至0.34，EEG觉醒从0.31提升至0.48，低通气从0.09提升至0.22，呼吸暂停从0.05提升至0.14。我们还报告了Brier分数和期望校准误差，其中完整融合模型在所有四项二元任务中取得了最佳校准。本研究表明，潜在几何/拓扑结构与EHR能够提供嵌入本身之外互补且可解释的信号，在极端不平衡条件下改善校准性和鲁棒性。

</details>


### [115] [Finite Sample Bounds for Learning with Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14168)
*Devin Smedira,Abhijith Jayakumar,Sidhant Misra,Marc Vuffray,Andrey Y. Lokhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对连续指数族分布的学习问题，提供了分数匹配方法的非渐近样本复杂度分析，首次推导出样本界对模型维度呈多项式依赖关系，填补了先前仅存在渐近界的研究空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 分数匹配因计算效率高而被广泛用于学习连续指数族分布，但其统计性质的理论理解尚不充分，尤其是样本复杂度的非渐近分析缺失。

Method: 采用非渐近样本复杂度分析方法，对基于分数匹配的多项式指数族结构学习进行理论推导。

Result: 导出的样本界对模型维度呈多项式依赖关系，这是首个此类非渐近样本复杂度界，所有先前工作仅展示了渐近界。

Conclusion: 分数匹配方法在多项式指数族结构学习中具有可保证的统计效率，且样本复杂度与模型维度呈多项式关系，优于仅能提供渐近保证的现有方法。

Original Abstract: Learning of continuous exponential family distributions with unbounded support remains an important area of research for both theory and applications in high-dimensional statistics. In recent years, score matching has become a widely used method for learning exponential families with continuous variables due to its computational ease when compared against maximum likelihood estimation. However, theoretical understanding of the statistical properties of score matching is still lacking. In this work, we provide a non-asymptotic sample complexity analysis for learning the structure of exponential families of polynomials with score matching. The derived sample bounds show a polynomial dependence on the model dimension. These bounds are the first of its kind, as all prior work has shown only asymptotic bounds on the sample complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 具有无界支撑的连续指数族分布的学习，仍然是高维统计中理论与应用的重要研究领域。近年来，分数匹配因其相较于极大似然估计在计算上的简便性，已成为学习连续变量指数族分布的广泛使用的方法。然而，分数匹配统计性质的理论理解仍然匮乏。本文针对分数匹配学习多项式指数族的结构问题，提供了非渐近的样本复杂度分析。导出的样本界表明对模型维度呈多项式依赖关系。这些界是同类中的首个，因为所有先前工作仅展示了样本复杂度的渐近界。

</details>


### [116] [LLMs Know When They Know, but Do Not Act on It: A Metacognitive Harness for Test-time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14186)
*Qi Cao,Yufan Wang,Peijia Qin,Shuhao Zhang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种元认知调控框架，将LLM的自我监控信号（求解前的感知知晓FOK和求解后的学习判断JOL）转化为显式的推理控制接口，在不更新参数的情况下显著提升了模型在文本、代码和多模态推理基准上的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LLM虽然能够产生自我监控信号（如自信度估计），但这些信号通常被孤立地测量或提取，而未用于控制推理过程。受认知心理学Nelson-Narens理论启发，作者希望探索LLM是否具备潜在的元认知能力，并将其转化为有效的测试时控制机制。

Method: 提出元认知调控框架，将监控与推理分离：模型先报告求解前的感知知晓（FOK）信号，再在每次求解尝试后报告学习判断（JOL）信号。利用这些信号构建显式控制接口，决定何时信任当前解、何时以紧凑的元认知反馈重试、以及何时将多次尝试传递给最终聚合器。

Result: 在文本、代码和多模态推理基准上，该框架在不更新参数或特定基准微调的情况下，将固定Claude Sonnet-4.6基模型的汇总准确率从48.3提升至56.9，并在HLE-Verified、LiveCodeBench v6和R-Bench-V三个主要评估设置上超过了最强的排行榜提交结果。

Conclusion: 强LLM可能已经具备有用的元认知能力，但需要显式的控制框架才能将其作用于推理过程。本文的元认知调控框架有效释放了这种潜在能力。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often expose useful signals of self-monitoring: before solving a problem, they can estimate whether they are likely to succeed, and after solving it, they can judge whether their answer is likely to be correct. However, these signals are typically measured or elicited in isolation, rather than used to control inference. In this work, we ask whether LLMs possess latent metacognitive ability that can be turned into effective test-time control. Inspired by the Nelson--Narens theory from cognitive psychology, we propose a metacognitive harness that separates monitoring from reasoning. For each problem, the model first reports a pre-solve feeling-of-knowing (FOK) signal; after each solve attempt, it reports a post-solve judgment-of-learning (JOL) signal. Rather than treating these signals as passive confidence estimates, the harness turns them into an explicit control interface for reasoning: it decides when to trust the current solution, when to retry with compact metacognitive feedback, and when to pass multiple attempts to a final aggregator. Across text, code, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks, our harness substantially improves a fixed Claude Sonnet-4.6 base model without parameter updates or benchmark-specific fine-tuning. On the evaluated public benchmark snapshots, it raises pooled accuracy from 48.3 to 56.9 and exceeds the strongest listed leaderboard entries on the three primary evaluation settings: HLE-Verified, LiveCodeBench v6, and R-Bench-V. These results suggest that strong LLMs may already possess useful metacognitive ability, but require an explicit control harness to act on it during reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）常常展现出有用的自我监控信号：在解决问题之前，它们可以估计自己是否可能成功；在解决问题之后，它们可以判断自己的答案是否可能正确。然而，这些信号通常是被孤立地测量或提取，而非用于控制推理过程。在这项工作中，我们探究LLM是否具备潜在的元认知能力，并能将其转化为有效的测试时控制。受认知心理学中Nelson-Narens理论的启发，我们提出了一种元认知调控框架，将监控与推理相分离。对于每个问题，模型首先报告求解前的感知知晓（FOK）信号；在每次求解尝试后，它再报告求解后的学习判断（JOL）信号。该框架并未将这些信号视为被动的置信度估计，而是将其转化为推理的显式控制接口：它决定何时信任当前解、何时以紧凑的元认知反馈进行重试、以及何时将多次尝试传递给最终聚合器。在文本、代码和多模态推理基准上，我们的框架在无参数更新或基准特定微调的情况下，显著提升了固定的Claude Sonnet-4.6基模型性能。在评估的公开基准快照上，它将汇总准确率从48.3提升至56.9，并在三个主要评估设置——HLE-Verified、LiveCodeBench v6和R-Bench-V——上超过了排行榜上最强的提交结果。这些结果表明，强大的LLM可能已经具备了有用的元认知能力，但需要显式的调控框架才能在推理过程中加以运用。

</details>


### [117] [Towards Fine-Grained and Verifiable Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14210)
*Yingying Fang,Haijie Xu,Shuang Wu,Mariathasan Anish,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种细粒度概念瓶颈模型（CBM）框架，将每个概念锚定在局部化的视觉证据上，在保持预测性能的同时显著提升可解释性和可验证性，使模型能够被直接检查是否学习了正确的概念而非虚假相关性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBM）虽提供了可解释性，但难以验证预测的概念是否对应正确的视觉证据，导致其可靠性受限，容易学习虚假相关性而非真正意图的概念。

Method: 提出细粒度CBM框架，将每个概念锚定在局部化的视觉证据上，使概念表征的位置和编码方式可直接被检查，从而允许用户解释预测并验证模型是否学习了预期概念。

Result: 在医学影像基准上，该框架学习到的概念空间信息完备，预测性能与标准CBM相当，同时显著提升了透明性；与事后归因方法不同，该方法能验证概念表征的存在性和正确性。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了可解释性与可验证性之间的差距，增强了CBM的信任度，为概念层面的人机交互建立了原则性机制，推动了更可靠且临床可操作的概念驱动学习系统的发展。

Original Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) offer interpretable alternatives to black-box predictors by introducing human-relatable concepts before the final output. However, existing CBMs struggle to verify whether predicted concepts correspond to the correct visual evidence, limiting their reliability. We propose a fine-grained CBM framework that grounds each concept in localized visual evidence, enabling direct inspection of where and how concepts are encoded. This design allows users to interpret predictions and verify that the model learns intended concepts rather than spurious correlations. Experiments on medical imaging benchmarks show that our learned concept space is information-complete and achieves predictive performance comparable to standard CBMs, while substantially improving transparency. Unlike post-hoc attribution methods, our framework validates both the presence and correctness of concept representations, bridging interpretability with verifiability. Our approach enhances the trustworthiness of CBMs and establishes a principled mechanism for human-model interaction at the concept level, paving the way toward more reliable and clinically actionable concept-based learning systems.

中文摘要翻译: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）通过在最终输出之前引入人类可理解的概念，为黑箱预测器提供了可解释的替代方案。然而，现有的CBM难以验证预测的概念是否对应于正确的视觉证据，限制了其可靠性。我们提出了一种细粒度的CBM框架，将每个概念锚定在局部化的视觉证据上，从而能够直接检查概念在哪里以及如何被编码。这种设计允许用户解释预测结果，并验证模型学习的是预期概念而非虚假相关性。在医学影像基准上的实验表明，我们学习到的概念空间是信息完备的，且实现了与标准CBM相当的预测性能，同时显著提升了透明性。与事后归因方法不同，我们的框架既验证了概念表征的存在性，也验证了其正确性，弥合了可解释性与可验证性之间的差距。我们的方法增强了CBM的可信度，并为概念层面的人机交互建立了一种原则性机制，为构建更可靠且具有临床可操作性的基于概念的学习系统铺平了道路。

</details>


### [118] [PreFT: Prefill-only finetuning for efficient inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14217)
*Andrew Lanpouthakoun,Aryaman Arora,Zhengxuan Wu,Dhruv Pai,Ben Keigwin,Dan Jurafsky,Christopher Potts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreFT（仅预填充微调），在推理时仅对预填充阶段的token应用适配器，解决了多适配器服务中解码阶段吞吐量低的问题，在保持性能接近的前提下显著提升吞吐量（512个适配器时吞吐量提升1.9倍）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法（PEFTs）在个性化服务多个用户适配器时，由于预填充阶段（批量处理大量token）与解码阶段（自回归生成单个token）之间存在吞吐量不匹配，即使使用专用内核和内存管理技术，解码阶段的吞吐量依然远低于预填充阶段，导致整体服务效率低下。因此，需要从参数数量优化转向服务吞吐量优化。

Method: 提出PreFT（Prefill-only Finetuning），仅在预填充阶段对token应用适配器，解码阶段丢弃适配器，从而消除解码阶段的吞吐瓶颈。在vLLM推理引擎上高效实现了两种仅预填充的PEFT方法：LoRA和ReFT。

Result: 1）服务多用户时，PreFT的吞吐量是传统PEFT的1.9倍（Llama 3.1 70B，512个适配器）；2）在监督微调（SFT）任务上，PreFT的评估损失略高于PEFT，但可通过增加秩来补偿，且几乎不降低吞吐量；3）在强化学习（RL）任务上，PreFT的性能与标准PEFT基本持平。

Conclusion: 仅预填充的LLM适配方法（PreFT）在准确率与吞吐量之间实现了比现有PEFT更优的权衡，验证了其在个性化服务场景中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Large language models can now be personalised efficiently at scale using parameter efficient finetuning methods (PEFTs), but serving user-specific PEFTs harms throughput, even with specialised kernels and memory management techniques. This is because, theoretically and empirically, a mismatch exists between prefill (processing a large number of tokens at once) and decode (generating a single token autoregressively): the latter has far lower throughput when serving multiple adapters. Rather than optimising performance relative to parameter count, for efficient multi-adapter serving, we instead ought to optimise performance relative to serving throughput. We therefore propose PreFT (Prefill-only Finetuning), wherein we only apply the adapter to prefill tokens and discard it afterwards.
